Find the letter for your own job on BMET's website and check that it says the same (how: see [Before you pay anyone](#before-you-pay-anyone)). On a single visa there is no such letter, so nothing official says who pays the flight out <sup>[N172 (2017)](#n172)</sup> (see Need to know below).

**You pay** about Tk 19,800 in Bangladesh-side fees, plus the skill test, a passport and any course (list below), and the agency may take only a capped amount (see [The legal maximum and what people really pay](#the-legal-maximum-and-what-people-really-pay)) <sup>[18](#o18), [N120](#n120), [19](#o19), [N121](#n121), [20](#o20), [21](#o21), [1](#o1)</sup>. The list:

- OEP registration: Tk 200 <sup>[18](#o18)</sup>.
- A TTC course admission fee, if you take a course: Tk 748 <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>.
- A passport: Tk 4,025 for a 48-page, 5-year e-passport, regular delivery <sup>[37](#o37)</sup>.
- The skill test, if your trade is listed: USD 85 per attempt (see [Step 3: Pass the skill test](#step-3-pass-the-skill-test)) <sup>[N9](#n9), [3](#o3)</sup>.
- The Wafid medical: agencies put it at about Tk 10,000 (2025), the USD 10 booking (about Tk 1,500 through a shop) plus about Tk 8,500 at the centre; the centre's official fee is not published (see [Step 4: Pass the Wafid medical check](#step-4-pass-the-wafid-medical-check)) <sup>[38](#o38), [N120](#n120)</sup>.
- Police clearance: Tk 1,500 (see [Step 6: Give the agency your papers for the visa (start before Step 5)](#step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-start-before-step-5)) <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>.
- Tasheer's service fee: about Tk 660-694, or about Tk 2,100-2,200 in its premium Lounge (see [Step 5: Give fingerprints at the Tasheer visa centre](#step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre)) <sup>[N120](#n120), [N9](#n9)</sup>.
- The pre-departure orientation (PDO): Tk 100 plus a Tk 100 platform charge (see [Step 8: Orientation, BMET clearance and the flight](#step-8-orientation-bmet-clearance-and-the-flight)) <sup>[N121](#n121)</sup>.
- The Wage Earners' Welfare Board fee: Tk 5,500 <sup>[20](#o20)</sup>.
- Migrant worker insurance: Tk 1,000, covering death or permanent disability for Tk 10 lakh over 5 years <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>.
- Source income tax at BMET clearance: Tk 500 for a Saudi single visa <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the medical centre's official fee for the tests?
> - Does BMET still charge Tk 250 for clearance and the smart card?
> - Does the DEMO charge for fingerprints?
> - On a single visa, who pays the outbound ticket: the employer or the worker?

<a id="the-legal-maximum-and-what-people-really-pay"></a>

#### 2.1.1 The legal maximum and what people really pay

**The rule**
The expatriates' ministry set a maximum of Tk 1.65 lakh in all in 2016-2017 and has not changed it; in October 2026 the state minister said new country-by-country maximums are coming <sup>[N8](#n8), [N173](#n173), [N174 (2024)](#n174)</sup>. Only group job orders need the ministry's recruitment permission (2017) <sup>[N172 (2017)](#n172)</sup>. In October 2025 the expatriates' welfare secretary, Neyamat Ullah Bhuiyan, said the government is pushing, through bilateral agreements, for workers to be sent at the employer's cost <sup>[N175](#n175)</sup>. Two BMET permission letters from September 2025 (RL-1059 and RL-0579) put every Saudi-side cost, levy and airfare included, on the employer, and let the agency take only what the letter allows, Tk 50,000 and Tk 58,610 respectively, by cheque or pay order <sup>[4](#o4), [25](#o25)</sup>. A worker on a single visa has no such letter, so only the Tk 1.65 lakh cap applies (how many go on single visas: see ["Is it open now, and who can go?"](#is-it-open-now-and-who-can-go), Channels) <sup>[N172 (2017)](#n172)</sup>. The fees a worker legitimately pays in Bangladesh are listed at the start of this section (see [Who pays what](#who-pays-what)).

**What people really pay**
In 2026 agencies put the whole cost at Tk 3.5-5 lakh and a press investigation at about Tk 5 lakh on average, up to Tk 9 lakh <sup>[N9](#n9), [N8](#n8)</sup>. In October 2026 the expatriates' welfare minister told agencies that where it is possible to go for Tk 3 lakh, taking Tk 7-8 lakh is improper, and urged them to stop collecting extra money from workers <sup>[N33](#n33)</sup>.

**Cases**

- November 2022: Nazrul Islam's father borrowed Tk 6 lakh from relatives to send him to Saudi Arabia as a cleaner; Nazrul sent home about Tk 1.5 lakh in his first seven to eight months, before his arrest (see [If you or a relative is arrested](#if-you-or-a-relative-is-arrested)), his father said <sup>[N176](#n176)</sup>.
- December 2025: Monowara Begum, a waste picker in Rajshahi city, said sending her elder son (26, SSC pass) through a broker cost Tk 9 lakh, that he gets "a lakh taka" in salary, and that he will come home in two years <sup>[N177](#n177)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Do new country maximums replace Tk 1.65 lakh?

<a id="if-you-need-to-borrow"></a>

#### 2.1.2 If you need to borrow

Probashi Kallyan Bank (PKB), the government bank for migrant workers, lends up to Tk 3 lakh without collateral at 9% simple interest, repaid monthly over up to 3 years including a grace period <sup>[22](#o22)</sup>. You can apply only once you hold the visa and the BMET smart card, which come near the end (see [Step 8: Orientation, BMET clearance and the flight](#step-8-orientation-bmet-clearance-and-the-flight)) <sup>[22](#o22)</sup>.

You'll need <sup>[22](#o22)</sup>:

- age 18 to 60 and no unpaid loan anywhere;
- 3 passport-size photos;
- copies of NID, passport, visa, BMET smart card (see [Step 8: Orientation, BMET clearance and the flight](#step-8-orientation-bmet-clearance-and-the-flight)) and contract if any;
- 3 signed MICR cheque leaves and a statement from your own bank account;
- Tk 500 to open a savings account;
- 2 guarantors who, like you, live permanently in the branch's area, each with a photo, NID copy and proof of address such as a utility bill:
  - one well-off person with 3 signed MICR cheque leaves, a bank statement and proof of income;
  - one family member or close relative.

1. At your nearest branch ([branch list](https://pkb.gov.bd/pages/static-pages/6922e0de933eb65569e28f0c)), fill in the free form and open the savings account <sup>[22](#o22), [39](#o39)</sup>.
2. Pay 0.5% processing and 0.5% documentation (each plus VAT) and Tk 900 per lakh risk cover; bring 16 revenue stamps of Tk 10, plus 3 adhesive stamps of Tk 100 for each guarantor <sup>[22](#o22)</sup>.
3. The bank must decide within 7 working days of complete papers <sup>[22](#o22)</sup>. Help: 16238, or +880 9677-787878 from abroad <sup>[22](#o22)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How, when and into which account is the loan paid?

**Cases**

- About 2019, seven years before August 2026: Mohon of Gondogoali, Atrai, Naogaon, went by mortgaging land and borrowing Tk 4 lakh from a samity (a local savings and loan association); by August 2026, when he and his brother died in a Riyadh factory fire, the debt had grown with interest to Tk 8 lakh and was still unpaid, his mother Moyna Begum said <sup>[N138](#n138)</sup>.
- 2026: Shahinur Rahman of Par-Naogaon, Naogaon, borrowed Tk 5.7 lakh from a samity against his homestead land at Par-Boalia and went about five months before he died in the August 2026 Riyadh fire, his widow Mim Begum said; she said the samity could take the land if the loan isn't repaid <sup>[N138](#n138)</sup>.

**Scam at this step:** PKB staff have been accused of asking Tk 10,000-20,000 in bribes for a Tk 3 lakh loan; there is no such fee <sup>[N178](#n178), [22](#o22)</sup>. Complain to PKB's grievance officer, the General Manager (Administration and Accounts), gm2@pkb.gov.bd, +88-02-48321195, who has 30 working days <sup>[40](#o40)</sup>; then the Deputy Managing Director, dmd@pkb.gov.bd, +88-02-48319468, who has 20 working days <sup>[40](#o40)</sup>. Or call the ministry hotline 16135 <sup>[21](#o21)</sup> or file at [grs.gov.bd](https://www.grs.gov.bd/), the government's complaint system <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-find-a-real-job"></a>

### 2.2 Step 1: Find a real job

You find a job in one of two ways: ask licensed agencies what Saudi jobs they have (item 1), or take a visa a relative or friend in Saudi Arabia offers (item 2). Single visas are often resold through brokers (2024) (how jobs reach workers: see ["Is it open now, and who can go?"](#is-it-open-now-and-who-can-go), Channels) <sup>[N45 (2024)](#n45)</sup>. Before paying anything, check the agency, the company and the visa (see [Before you pay anyone](#before-you-pay-anyone)) <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>. Register on OEP first (see While you look, item 1) <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-1-find-a-real-job-item-1"></a>**Ask licensed agencies what Saudi jobs they have, and check each one.** Recruiting without a licence under the Overseas Employment and Migrants Act 2013 is not allowed, and travel agencies may not send workers <sup>[41 (2013)](#o41), [N179](#n179)</sup>.

   You'll need: the agency's name or RL (recruiting licence) number.

   1. Search the name or RL number on [OEP's agency list](https://www.oep.gov.bd/agencies); the row shows licence status, validity date, office address and phone <sup>[42](#o42)</sup>. Go no further unless it says "Active" and the date hasn't passed (others are Expired, Suspended, Cancelled; licences are cancelled for visa fraud) <sup>[42](#o42), [N179](#n179)</sup>.
   2. Ask the agency, at that address and phone, which Saudi jobs they have for your trade, and ask to see the employer's demand letter (company, jobs, pay), with the embassy's attestation if it is for more than one worker (see [Step 2: See the attested demand letter](#step-2-see-the-attested-demand-letter)) <sup>[42](#o42), [21](#o21), [N53](#n53)</sup>.
   3. Read workers' reviews on [Recruitment Advisor](https://www.recruitmentadvisor.org/search-an-agency), a trade-union site, giving what each paid, for which country, and whether they got the promised job and pay <sup>[N180](#n180), [N181](#n181)</sup>.

   **Which agencies.** Only Bonanza Overseas (RL0579) has outside evidence of fair recruitment (its entry is below) <sup>[N182 (2022)](#n182)</sup>; the others are listed by how many Saudi workers BMET approved for them, not by quality, and some carry red flags. No official grading of agencies exists, and the state minister for expatriates' welfare has said he could not confidently recommend any agency <sup>[N183](#n183)</sup>. These had the most Saudi workers in the 1,148 permission letters BMET posted from June 2023 to December 2025 (70,145 workers from 370 agencies, counted by the team; workers means posts approved, not people who went); a high count shows activity, not fair treatment <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>. Red flags are allegations or investigations, not court verdicts; the full list of agencies and flags is in the knowledge base's [agencies folder](https://github.com/FiazAbrar/bsaisl-migration-knowledge-base/tree/main/agencies).

   - **Greenland Overseas (RL0040, Active):** 44 letters, 7,421 workers <sup>[16](#o16), [42](#o42)</sup>. Red flag: the ACC (Anti-Corruption Commission) filed a case in September 2025 against its owners, one of 13 agencies accused of forming a syndicate that charged Malaysia-bound workers nearly five times the government rate <sup>[N184](#n184)</sup>.
   - **Career Overseas Consultants (RL0403, Active):** 24 letters, 3,757 workers <sup>[16](#o16), [42](#o42)</sup>. Red flag: Bloomberg (2026) reports agents' allegations that, during Malaysia's 2016-2018 hiring round, it and the other nine agencies Malaysia routed hiring through charged workers a syndicate fee of about USD 1,350; it denies collecting unlawful fees, and a 2018 lawsuit against the 10 agencies was withdrawn with no findings of wrongdoing <sup>[N185](#n185), [N135 (2024)](#n135)</sup>.
   - **A.J. International (RL1304, Active):** 26 letters, 2,755 workers <sup>[16](#o16), [42](#o42)</sup>.
   - **Bashundhara Employment Services (RL1064, Active):** 12 letters, 2,090 workers <sup>[16](#o16), [42](#o42)</sup>.
   - **Amco Enterprise (RL1537, Active):** 28 letters, 1,933 workers <sup>[16](#o16), [42](#o42)</sup>.
   - **United Gulf Services (RL1036, Active):** 23 letters, 1,850 workers <sup>[16](#o16), [42](#o42)</sup>. Red flag: Prothom Alo (September 2026) reports it is on Malaysia's 2026 list of 25 agencies and is owned by the wife of an owner of Greenland Overseas, which was in the earlier Malaysia syndicate; no allegation is made against United Gulf itself <sup>[N186](#n186)</sup>.
   - **Bonanza Overseas (RL0579, Active, +88028881118):** 21 letters, 1,642 workers <sup>[16](#o16), [42](#o42)</sup>. The only one with outside evidence of fair recruitment: it was the Bangladeshi agency in an ILO-backed fair recruitment pilot for construction jobs in Qatar (2018-2021), not Saudi Arabia <sup>[N182 (2022)](#n182)</sup>. BMET capped its cost at Tk 58,610 per worker in one Saudi letter <sup>[25](#o25)</sup>.
   - **Azam Overseas (RL0535, Active):** 10 letters, 1,221 workers <sup>[16](#o16), [42](#o42)</sup>.

   In July 2026 the ministry revoked 49 licences in the Malaysia syndicate case, among them Catharsis International (RL0549), Rabbi International (RL0258) and Sultan Overseas (RL0906), which held Saudi letters for 971, 936 and 887 workers; OEP shows them Cancelled <sup>[N187](#n187), [16](#o16), [42](#o42)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Which other agencies place workers in the promised Saudi job at a fair cost?

   **Brokers.** Since 2023 an agency may appoint only a person registered under the rules as its sub-agent <sup>[17 (2023)](#o17)</sup>. Pay the agency itself through its bank account (see [Before you pay anyone](#before-you-pay-anyone)), not the broker <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>, and write down the broker's name, age, parents' names and address, which a BMET complaint asks for <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Is there a public list of registered sub-agents to check a broker against?
   > - Must the agency repay money its broker took?

2. <a id="step-1-find-a-real-job-item-2"></a>**If a relative or friend in Saudi Arabia offers you a visa.** A visa for one or a few workers is a single visa (সিঙ্গেল ভিসা); one that comes through someone abroad is also called a personal or FNF (friends and family) visa (single and group visas, and how many go this way: see ["Is it open now, and who can go?"](#is-it-open-now-and-who-can-go), Channels) <sup>[N51](#n51), [N14 (2020)](#n14)</sup>. Even a visa that came free from family must then be carried by a Bangladeshi agency the Saudi embassy has approved (what to do: see [If a relative or friend offers you a visa](#if-a-relative-or-friend-offers-you-a-visa)) <sup>[6](#o6)</sup>.

<a id="before-you-pay-anyone"></a>

#### 2.2.1 Before you pay anyone

Pay nothing until the agency (item 1), the company (below) and the visa ([Step 7: Check the visa yourself](#step-7-check-the-visa-yourself)) check out, and pay the bulk only after the visa is issued, BMET clearance is done and the flight is confirmed <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. Pay the agency through its bank account, by cheque, bank draft or pay order, and keep the receipt <sup>[21](#o21), [4](#o4)</sup>; complaints without proof of payment fail at BMET and at legal aid, or win little compensation <sup>[N188 (2019)](#n188), [N189 (2024)](#n189)</sup>.

- **Get the employer's name, job, salary and embassy-attested contract in writing,** and the agency's name, licence number, address and phone <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>.

- **Check the company exists.** Saudi companies have a unified national number starting with 7, which replaced the commercial registration (CR) number <sup>[44 (2020)](#o44), [45](#o45)</sup>.

  You'll need: the company's name or unified national number.

  1. On [mc.gov.sa/en/eservices/Pages/Commercial-data.aspx](https://mc.gov.sa/en/eservices/Pages/Commercial-data.aspx) (Ministry of Commerce, free, in Arabic), search the name or number, type the verification code and press Search; the registration data appear at once <sup>[45](#o45)</sup>. Help: 1900 in Saudi Arabia, cs@mc.gov.sa from Bangladesh <sup>[45](#o45)</sup>.
  2. If the company isn't found or doesn't match the employer on your demand letter, don't pay, and complain about the agency to BMET (see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money)) <sup>[45](#o45), [21](#o21), [1](#o1)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Which fields does the result show?

- **Look for BMET's permission letter for your job.** A worker on a single visa won't find a letter (see [The legal maximum and what people really pay](#the-legal-maximum-and-what-people-really-pay)), so for them the DEMO check (next point) is the one that counts <sup>[N172 (2017)](#n172)</sup>. A letter names the agency and RL number, the Saudi company and sponsor ID, the visa and demand numbers, the job, the number of workers, salary and benefits, and the most the agency may take from each worker <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>.

  You'll need: the agency's name or RL number.

  1. On [bmet.gov.bd/pages/miscellaneous-infos](https://bmet.gov.bd/pages/miscellaneous-infos), search the agency's name, RL number or "Saudi" <sup>[46](#o46), [47](#o47)</sup>.
  2. Open that agency's Saudi PDF and compare company, job, salary and maximum cost with what you were told <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. One letter, for example, allowed at most Tk 50,000, by cheque, bank draft or pay order only, after visa, clearance and flight were confirmed, with the employer paying all Saudi-side costs and each worker signing the contract and getting a Bangla translation <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>.
  3. No letter for your job? Have the papers checked at your DEMO (next point) <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Does BMET still issue these letters for Saudi group visas, and where?

- **Have your papers checked.** Take copies of the demand letter, contract and visa to your DEMO or BMET (89/2 Kakrail, Dhaka-1000) and ask them to check they are real <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>; DEMO addresses and phones are in BMET's list of DEMOs (see [Step 1: Find a real job](#step-1-find-a-real-job), While you look, item 1) <sup>[48](#o48), [49](#o49)</sup>. The visa check is free and takes 3 working days (see [Step 7, item 2: At BMET or your DEMO](#step-7-check-the-visa-yourself-item-2)) <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Is the check of a demand letter and contract also free, and how long does it take?
  > - Is the answer in writing?

- **If you need to borrow:** see [If you need to borrow](#if-you-need-to-borrow).

- **If the agency, a broker or a relative takes your money:** see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money).

<a id="if-a-relative-or-friend-offers-you-a-visa"></a>

#### 2.2.2 If a relative or friend offers you a visa

**Check the visa and the sponsor yourself before any money moves (item 1 below).** The paper doesn't show where the visa came from. A relative's visa is one of three things, and all three arrive as the same work visa <sup>[N43 (2024)](#n43), [6](#o6)</sup>:

- *A real job with the relative's own employer.* The employer pays the visa, recruitment fees, the iqama and their renewals, so the visa itself should cost you nothing <sup>[50 (2023)](#o50), [35](#o35)</sup>. Relatives still charge: one migrant paid a close relative Tk 4 lakh for a visa the relative had got free <sup>[N190 (2022)](#n190)</sup>.
- *A visa the relative bought from a kafil (the sponsor) or a visa trader.* It has already cost about Tk 2 lakh before it leaves Saudi Arabia (see ["How a job reaches you, and who takes a cut"](#how-a-job-reaches-you-and-who-takes-a-cut), The root) <sup>[N8](#n8)</sup>, and the sponsor will pass his yearly levy and iqama fee to you (see If it turns out to be a free visa, below). These are the free (azad) visas, meaning you find your own work <sup>[N56](#n56)</sup>, and new ones are issued as amel adi (ordinary labourer) or load-unload (লোড আনলোড) visas, because load-unload needs no skill test <sup>[N191](#n191), [N192](#n192), [N120](#n120)</sup>. The job may not be the one promised: a cousin paid Tk 4 lakh for a driver visa and the employer put the worker to herding goats, then camels <sup>[N190 (2022)](#n190)</sup>.
- *A visa the relative sells as a business.* A returnee broker in Cumilla charged Tk 3 lakh a head and passed most of it to the friends in the Gulf who supplied the visas <sup>[N157 (2024)](#n157)</sup>. Many men present a relative as the source when they really bought from a broker <sup>[N193 (2024)](#n193)</sup>.

Some are free visas or household visas with no real job (2020, 2025) <sup>[N54 (2020)](#n54), [N191](#n191)</sup>. Item 1 tells you which one you have.

**You'll need from the relative, before any money moves** <sup>[N142 (2024)](#n142), [51](#o51), [8](#o8), [45](#o45), [N194](#n194)</sup>:

- the 10-digit visa number;
- the sponsor's name and his Saudi ID, iqama number or company registration (CR) number;
- the occupation on the visa;
- whether the sponsor is a company, an establishment or a household;
- the wage;
- the name and RL (recruiting licence) number of the Bangladeshi agency that will hold the visa.

1. <a id="if-a-relative-or-friend-offers-you-a-visa-item-1"></a>**Check the visa and the sponsor before paying anything.** On [ksavisa.sa/track-application](https://ksavisa.sa/track-application), the Saudi Foreign Ministry's visa platform, enter the visa number with the sponsor's Saudi ID, iqama or CR number (the inviter's number) and the captcha <sup>[51](#o51)</sup>. Check a company on the Ministry of Commerce site by its name or unified national number (see [Step 1: Find a real job](#step-1-find-a-real-job), Before you pay anyone) <sup>[45](#o45)</sup>. For a household visa, the relative can have the sponsor check Musaned's Visa Status Inquiry with the sponsor's ID and the request number while you are on a call <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. Your own passport number shows nothing useful until the agency files the MOFA (see item 5.2), which attaches your passport to the visa <sup>[N120](#n120), [N104 (2022)](#n104)</sup>.

   *The trap:* a PDF or photo on WhatsApp proves nothing. In Bishwanath, Sylhet, a family with a brother in Saudi Arabia took Tk 5 lakh for a driving visa and handed over a PDF of a different, three-month visa <sup>[N195](#n195)</sup>.

   *What it tells you:* if the sponsor is a person or household rather than a company, it is a household visa: you'll be a domestic worker, outside the Labour Law, working only for that household, not at the relative's company <sup>[7](#o7), [N54 (2020)](#n54)</sup>. These are the classic free visas <sup>[N54 (2020)](#n54)</sup>: a Saudi household pays no levy on its first four domestic workers, so the visa costs it nothing a year to keep in levy <sup>[N196 (2023)](#n196)</sup>. A load-unload or amel adi occupation carries the same warning <sup>[N191](#n191)</sup>.

   *If it doesn't match* what you were promised (another sponsor, another job, no record), don't pay. Visas can also vanish when the labour ministry cancels applications filed on fake demand letters (see ["Is it open now, and who can go?"](#is-it-open-now-and-who-can-go), Current situation) <sup>[N13](#n13)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Before the MOFA, does ksavisa.sa track-application show a work visa's sponsor and occupation from the visa number and the sponsor's ID or CR number, or nothing?

2. <a id="if-a-relative-or-friend-offers-you-a-visa-item-2"></a>**Get the agency's name from the relative, check it, and get proof it holds the visa.** The worker rarely chooses: the okala goes to whatever agency the visa seller already deals with, sometimes one whose licence he rents <sup>[N45 (2024)](#n45)</sup>. The sponsor files the okala (Arabic tafweed or wakala) on ksavisa.sa (still called Enjaz) under Authorisation on a visa, entering the visa number, his unified number, the agency's name, your city of arrival and the number of visas <sup>[53](#o53), [N194](#n194), [N104 (2022)](#n104)</sup>. It takes one working day and the government fee starts at SR 75 (Tk 2,500); Saudi service firms charge about SR 200 to file it <sup>[53](#o53), [N194](#n194)</sup>. This is the sponsor's cost, not yours <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>.

   Check the agency's RL number on OEP: "Active", with a validity date not passed (see [Step 1, item 1: Ask licensed agencies what Saudi jobs they have, and check each one](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-1)) <sup>[42](#o42)</sup>. Then, before paying the agency anything, get it to confirm in writing, or by a screenshot from its own login on the Saudi visa platform, that it holds the okala for your visa number <sup>[14](#o14)</sup>. Later, check that the same agency appears on your MOFA printout (see item 5.2).

   *The trap:* once the okala is filed you are stuck with that agency. Moving means the first agency cancels both the okala and the MOFA, which costs money and time, doesn't always work, and often leaves the passport unusable for a new visa <sup>[N197](#n197)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What does it cost in 2026 to cancel an okala and MOFA and move to another agency?
   > - Is the office that "processes" a single visa often a sub-agent's, not the agency named on the okala?

3. <a id="if-a-relative-or-friend-offers-you-a-visa-item-3"></a>**Make sure the demand letter will be attested.** Since 22 December 2024 the employer's demand letter must be attested by the Bangladesh Embassy in Riyadh if it is for more than one worker, whether a group visa or a single visa for a few workers; a demand letter for one worker needs no embassy attestation (see [The Bangladesh embassy's attestation](#the-bangladesh-embassys-attestation-and-the-missing-contract), and the missing contract) <sup>[N53](#n53), [N52](#n52), [N109](#n109)</sup>. The embassy attests online on beriyadh.org for registered companies (see [Step 2: See the attested demand letter](#step-2-see-the-attested-demand-letter)) <sup>[54](#o54)</sup>. A sponsor who won't attest is a warning: companies that bring workers only to let them loose without an iqama are slow to give a demand letter <sup>[N198](#n198)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - In November 2026, does BMET clear a one-person Saudi visa without the attested demand letter?
   > - How does a household sponsor, who can't register on beriyadh.org as a company, get one attested?

4. <a id="if-a-relative-or-friend-offers-you-a-visa-item-4"></a>**Pay in stages you can check, and so you can prove it.** The relative will usually want money first, because he has already paid the kafil in riyals; your family pays taka at home to his family, outside the banks, and part leaves by hundi (হুন্ডি, informal money transfer) <sup>[N199 (2022)](#n199), [N8](#n8)</sup>. Tie each payment to something you have checked yourself: nothing until the visa and sponsor check out (item 1) and the agency confirms it holds the okala (item 2); a part once the MOFA printout shows the promised sponsor and agency (item 5.2); the rest only once the e-visa is issued and checked (see [Step 7: Check the visa yourself](#step-7-check-the-visa-yourself)). Pay by bank or bKash to a named account, never cash: in a Banshkhali case a Saudi-based man took Tk 3.58 lakh through his wife for a visa he never sent, and police confirmed the fraud from the bank and bKash records <sup>[N200](#n200)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Do families take a stamp-paper deed or a security cheque from a relative selling a visa?
   > - Has either ever won a case?

5. <a id="if-a-relative-or-friend-offers-you-a-visa-item-5"></a>**Let the agency carry the file, in this order.** From medical to flight usually takes 1.5-2.5 months, a month at the fastest <sup>[N120](#n120)</sup>.

   1. *Medical:* book the Wafid slip yourself (see [Step 4: Pass the Wafid medical check](#step-4-pass-the-wafid-medical-check)). Agencies say you can't, and take it for about Tk 1,500 (USD 10 plus about Tk 200 for the shop) <sup>[N192](#n192), [N120](#n120)</sup>.
   2. *MOFA (মোফা):* after a fit result the agency files your visa application on Enjaz with your passport and photo; from then a search shows the sponsor's name and the application number (see [Step 7: Check the visa yourself](#step-7-check-the-visa-yourself)) <sup>[N120](#n120), [N104 (2022)](#n104)</sup>. Check the sponsor and agency on it before paying the next part. Drop out after MOFA and the agency charges a "MOFA fine", usually Tk 3,000 <sup>[N120](#n120)</sup>. Some agencies won't file until you bring police clearance, so apply early (see [Step 6: Give the agency your papers for the visa (start before Step 5)](#step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-start-before-step-5)) <sup>[N120](#n120)</sup>.
   3. *Skill test,* if the occupation is a listed trade (see [Step 3: Pass the skill test](#step-3-pass-the-skill-test)) <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>. It can also come before the medical, since the certificate lasts five years <sup>[N201 (2023)](#n201)</sup>.
   4. *Tasheer fingerprints:* the agency books the slip (what it costs and what the agency may charge: see [Step 5: Give fingerprints at the Tasheer visa centre](#step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre)). Once all papers reach the Saudi mission the e-visa can be issued in 3-4 days; agencies quote a week to two months <sup>[N192](#n192), [N120](#n120)</sup>. Check the e-visa yourself (see [Step 7: Check the visa yourself](#step-7-check-the-visa-yourself)) <sup>[N142 (2024)](#n142), [1](#o1)</sup>.
   5. *Orientation and BMET's own fingerprint:* the 3-day orientation (see [Step 8: Orientation, BMET clearance and the flight](#step-8-orientation-bmet-clearance-and-the-flight)), then a second fingerprint, BMET's bio-enrolment ("bio finger"), for the clearance card <sup>[N120](#n120)</sup>.
   6. *BMET clearance (ম্যানপাওয়ার):* the agency applies, in 3 working days (see [Step 8: Orientation, BMET clearance and the flight](#step-8-orientation-bmet-clearance-and-the-flight)) <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>. BMET's charter lets a worker who got a visa on his own apply alone <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>, but after the okala the visa sits in the approved agency's name, so the agency handles the okala, MOFA and Tasheer and in practice the clearance too <sup>[6](#o6), [N104 (2022)](#n104)</sup>.
   7. *Payment and ticket:* you settle the agency's bill before it takes the clearance and buys the ticket <sup>[N120](#n120)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Since September 2025, has BMET's emigration branch cleared a Saudi single visa for a worker who applied without an agency?

   *The risk of delay:* a visa that sits too long during attestation, the medical or Tasheer can lapse, and the seller then asks for money for a new one.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How long does a Saudi work visa stay usable after issue on Qiwa?
   > - What do sellers charge when it lapses before the worker flies?

6. <a id="if-a-relative-or-friend-offers-you-a-visa-item-6"></a>**Know who answers if it fails.** The visa seller signs the agency a written undertaking (মুচলেকা, muchleka) taking all liability for the workers, so on a single visa the agency is liable for nothing beyond the clearance papers <sup>[N45 (2024)](#n45)</sup>. The agency got perhaps Tk 20,000 of the Tk 4-5 lakh, so a BMET case against it recovers little, and single-visa workers who come back without work get back not even Tk 1 lakh <sup>[N45 (2024)](#n45)</sup>. If you named a relative as the source but bought from a broker, there is no one in Bangladesh to pursue <sup>[N193 (2024)](#n193)</sup>. What to do if the relative cheats you: see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money), If the relative cheats you.

7. <a id="if-a-relative-or-friend-offers-you-a-visa-item-7"></a>**After arrival.** In the first week set up Absher and check your Qiwa contract against what you were promised (see [Set up Absher and check your Qiwa contract](#set-up-absher-and-check-your-qiwa-contract)). Every month check your iqama expiry and any "Absent from Work" flag on Absher (see ["If your employer reports you absent (huroob)"](#if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-huroob)) <sup>[N130](#n130), [N202](#n202)</sup>. Keep your passport; the sponsor may not hold it (see [Your passport](#your-passport-1)) <sup>[23](#o23)</sup>. The iqama, permit, levy, renewals and any transfer fee (SR 2,000 for a first transfer) are the employer's <sup>[35](#o35), [9](#o9), [N148](#n148)</sup>. If no iqama comes or the job isn't there, see If it turns out to be a free visa, below.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What does a free-visa worker pay a broker or kafil in 2026 to transfer his sponsorship to a real employer?

**What it costs, official against real.** A relative's visa is not cheaper than an agency's: workers paid relatives Tk 286,642 on average, brokers Tk 303,351 and agencies Tk 243,531 <sup>[N42 (2022)](#n42)</sup>.

- *Genuine company visa:* you pay only the Bangladesh-side fees, about Tk 19,800 in all (the list under Who pays what), before the skill test, passport and ticket <sup>[18](#o18), [N120](#n120), [19](#o19), [N121](#n121), [20](#o20), [21](#o21), [1](#o1)</sup>. In practice the agency's processing fee is whatever you negotiate <sup>[N192](#n192)</sup>, and a visa seller can rent an agency's licence for Tk 15,000-20,000 a visa to get the clearance <sup>[N45 (2024)](#n45)</sup>.
- *Free visa:* Tk 3-5 lakh through an agency and up to Tk 5-7 lakh through a broker <sup>[N203](#n203), [N56](#n56)</sup>; another 2026 account puts the whole trip at Tk 8-9 lakh <sup>[N204](#n204)</sup>, and in July 2026 two relatives of a Cumilla worker paid one man Tk 12 lakh between them, with Tk 4 lakh more due after arrival <sup>[N204](#n204)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - In 2026, what does a Dhaka agency charge in total to process a Saudi visa the worker brings (okala, MOFA, Tasheer, clearance, ticket)?
> - What is that package called on the street?
> - What does the ticket cost with taxes, against the USD 360 worker base fare <sup>[N205](#n205)</sup>?
> - What does a free visa cost in all in 2026: Tk 3-7 lakh or Tk 8-9 lakh?

**Women.** A woman going as a domestic worker should pay nothing and goes through a ministry-approved agency and Musaned ([Work visa: domestic worker](#work-visa-domestic-worker)) <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>. A relative arranging it is a warning: in October 2026 a Muktagachha woman's cousin met her at the Saudi airport, had her sign an Arabic paper and, she says, had "sold" her to a sponsor for two years on a khadema (খাদেমা, housemaid) visa at SR 1,000 a month; he was arrested under the human trafficking law <sup>[N206](#n206)</sup>.

**If it turns out to be a free visa**

*The iqama traps.* By law the employer gets and pays for your iqama within 90 days of arrival, and a late one costs the employer a SR 500 fine, not you <sup>[23](#o23), [N207 (2022)](#n207), [9](#o9)</sup>. On a bought visa it rarely works that way. A three-month iqama is legal, but it is the classic sign of a free visa: if no job turns up before it runs out, you are illegal <sup>[N207 (2022)](#n207), [N43 (2024)](#n43)</sup>. Nine men from Gurudaspur, Natore, each paid a villager settled in Saudi Arabia Tk 4-5 lakh; two years later none had an iqama, and his wife said it was because they hadn't paid extra for it <sup>[N208 (2023)](#n208)</sup>. In May 2025 the uncle of Sajib, of Talson, Adamdighi, Bogura, said the company that Motiur Rahman, a manpower trader from Raninagar, Naogaon, had sent Sajib to had still not given him an iqama, and that Motiur had promised one and kept making excuses; family sources told Prothom Alo that Sajib had gone two years earlier and got no iqama because of problems with his papers, while Sajib's father said the next day that he had sent him 40 days earlier (see [The village end](#the-village-end)) <sup>[N209](#n209), [N112](#n112)</sup>. On a free visa the kafil passes the levy and iqama fee to you (see ["What you pay, and what you earn and keep"](#what-you-pay-and-what-you-earn-and-keep), On a free visa you pay it yourself) <sup>[N128](#n128), [N127 (2024)](#n127)</sup>. In 2026 kafils took SR 16,000-18,000 a year for making and renewing the iqama <sup>[N210](#n210)</sup>; one 2025 account puts the yearly cut, called faida (ফায়দা), at SR 10,000-12,000 on average, rising as the years go on <sup>[N191](#n191)</sup>. Anything above the levy and fees, or any levy demanded from a company worker, is the sponsor's cut. Brokers have sold a "one-year" iqama for SR 5,000 and handed over a three-month one <sup>[N210](#n210)</sup>. One worker flown home from a Saudi jail in November 2026 had paid a middleman Tk 3.7 lakh for an iqama that was never made <sup>[N211](#n211)</sup>.

*The sponsor's weapons:* working for anyone but the sponsor breaks Article 39 of the Labour Law, a SR 10,000 fine and deportation for the worker the first time <sup>[50 (2023)](#o50), [5 (2024)](#o5)</sup>; since February 2026 a business that lets its workers work for others faces up to SR 100,000 <sup>[N212](#n212)</sup>. So a sponsor whose worker is caught outside reports him absent (huroob), and one worker-writer described kafils filing theft cases, sometimes for over a crore taka, which stop the worker going home <sup>[N213 (2023)](#n213)</sup>. Don't sign or thumbprint blank or unexplained papers for the kafil <sup>[N213 (2023)](#n213)</sup>.

*If it fails, on a company visa:* no iqama after 90 days, complain on 19911 ([After you arrive](#after-you-arrive)) <sup>[23](#o23)</sup>. You can move employer without consent once your work permit has expired or was never issued <sup>[N140](#n140)</sup>, issue your own exit and re-entry or final exit visa on Absher or Qiwa <sup>[28](#o28)</sup>, and if reported absent you have 60 days to transfer or take a final exit <sup>[31 (2022)](#o31)</sup>.

*If it fails, on a household visa:* only the domestic worker rules apply. The ministry can move you to another employer if your iqama isn't issued or renewed within 30 days <sup>[30 (2024)](#o30)</sup>, but you cannot issue your own final exit visa; the employer does it on Absher, which is the kafil's real hold over free-visa men <sup>[N145](#n145)</sup>.

*Either way:* the embassy's online form has a "no iqama after arrival" option, and the Special Exit Programme covers workers whose iqama was never issued ([Going home without valid papers](#going-home-without-valid-papers)) <sup>[55](#o55), [8](#o8)</sup>. Until the end of 2026 an employer can still regularise a permit never issued within 6 months of joining <sup>[N163](#n163)</sup>.

<a id="while-you-look-register-on-oep-and-learn-a-trade"></a>

#### 2.2.3 While you look: register on OEP and learn a trade

1. <a id="while-you-look-register-on-oep-and-learn-a-trade-item-1"></a>**Register on the Overseas Employment Platform (OEP),** BMET's website for applying for TTC training, the compulsory 3-day pre-departure orientation and BMET clearance <sup>[21](#o21), [56](#o56)</sup>.

   You'll need <sup>[56](#o56), [36](#o36)</sup>:

   - your NID (national ID card);
   - a mobile phone with that number;
   - a bKash, Nagad or other mobile banking account with Tk 202;
   - a recent clear photo that matches your face today.

   1. In Google Chrome open [employee.oep.gov.bd/employee/login-with-otp](https://employee.oep.gov.bd/employee/login-with-otp) (or scan the QR code on a BMET or TTC poster), go to registration and type every detail exactly as on your NID, without dots in your name; verify with the OTP (one-time code) sent to your phone, then Submit and "Save and continue" <sup>[56](#o56)</sup>.
   2. Fill in the rest, choose Mobile Banking at payment, then bKash, Nagad or another service, and enter your account number, OTP and PIN; the fee is Tk 200 (Tk 202 with the charge), paid only online inside OEP <sup>[56](#o56), [18](#o18), [36](#o36)</sup>.
   3. Download the Registration Card <sup>[56](#o56)</sup>. Take 2 printed copies of the registration slip to your district employment and manpower office (DEMO, BMET's office in each district) to give fingerprints <sup>[18](#o18)</sup>; addresses, phones and emails are in BMET's [list of DEMOs](https://bmet.gov.bd/pages/static-pages/6922e139933eb65569e2af6c) <sup>[48](#o48), [49](#o49)</sup>.

   Already registered? Log in with your mobile number and OTP <sup>[36](#o36), [56](#o56)</sup>. Problems: OEP hotline 09614445271 <sup>[57](#o57)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What does the DEMO give you after the fingerprints?
   > - How do you correct a wrong name or date of birth?
   > - What scams target workers at this step (fake registration sites, shops charging extra)?

2. <a id="while-you-look-register-on-oep-and-learn-a-trade-item-2"></a>**Learn a trade and get a certificate.** Workers in the trades Saudi Arabia lists must pass the Takamol skill test (see [Step 3: Pass the skill test](#step-3-pass-the-skill-test)), which expects real skill and work experience in the trade <sup>[11](#o11), [58](#o58)</sup>. BMET's Technical Training Centres (TTCs) run 3-month courses ending in a BNQF (Bangladesh National Qualifications Framework, the national skills standard) assessment <sup>[36](#o36), [59](#o59)</sup>; BMET's [trades page](https://bmet.gov.bd/pages/static-pages/6922dc3c933eb65569e0f2b7) lists the trades and seats at every TTC <sup>[60](#o60)</sup>.

   You'll need <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>:

   - your OEP registration;
   - an SSC pass or a JSC/class 8 pass, depending on the course;
   - age 18 to 40 (21 to 40 for driving);
   - Tk 748.

   1. Watch your TTC's website for the next session's admission notice, for example [TTC Gaibandha's notice board](https://ttc.gaibandha.gov.bd/pages/notices/6a1171662de6aeac14f7e3b4) <sup>[59](#o59)</sup>. Notices come out weeks before each three-month session, and applications stay open about four weeks (TTC Magura: 18 August to 15 September for October-December) <sup>[59](#o59), [36](#o36)</sup>.
   2. In OEP go to Training portal, Training, Trade/Skills, your division, district and institution, Search <sup>[56](#o56), [59](#o59)</sup>; select the course, then Apply; if your photo matches your face today press Continue, then Process to Enroll and Confirm <sup>[56](#o56)</sup>. Applications are capped at three times the seats <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>.
   3. Sit the written admission test at the TTC on the notice date (Bangla, English, maths, general science, overseas jobs, visas and labour law) <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>.
   4. When an SMS says you passed, pay in OEP under Training Portal, My Course, Admission Fee: Tk 748; the driving course is free and includes the licence fee <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>.

      > **Need to know**
      > - What happens if you fail the admission test or miss the fee deadline?
      > - Can you apply for the next session?

   5. Attend at least 80% of classes and sit the final assessment; competent trainees get Tk 150 a day for travel and food (Tk 100 for driving) when the budget allows <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>. The TTC helps with job placement <sup>[59](#o59)</sup>.

<a id="step-2-see-the-attested-demand-letter"></a>

### 2.3 Step 2: See the attested demand letter

The demand letter is the employer's request for workers. If it is for more than one worker, the employer, not you, has it attested by the Bangladesh Embassy in Riyadh; a letter for one worker needs no embassy attestation <sup>[54](#o54), [N53](#n53)</sup>. You ask the agency for a copy ([Step 2, item 1: Ask the agency for a copy of the demand letter, embassy-attested if it is for more than one worker, and the contract](#step-2-see-the-attested-demand-letter-item-1)) <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>.

How the employer gets it: it registers on the Bangladesh Embassy Riyadh's e-demand system at [beriyadh.org](https://beriyadh.org/employers/create) with its company registration number and uploads its demand letter, a power of attorney naming the agency, the employment contract, a letter of undertaking, a letter of guarantee, its project papers and its wage protection certificate, with a list of at least 10 current workers <sup>[54](#o54)</sup>. The embassy's labour wing checks and attests them online, for group visas and for single visas for more than one worker <sup>[61](#o61), [N109](#n109), [N53](#n53)</sup>. Asif Nazrul, the interim government's expatriates' welfare adviser, launched this e-Demand Attestation System in January 2025, so Saudi companies no longer have to go to the embassy <sup>[N124](#n124)</sup>.

**An attested letter doesn't prove the job is real:** some are attested without checks or for bribes <sup>[N8](#n8)</sup>. Check the company (see [Step 1: Find a real job](#step-1-find-a-real-job), Before you pay anyone) and the visa (see [Step 7: Check the visa yourself](#step-7-check-the-visa-yourself)) too <sup>[45](#o45), [51](#o51)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-2-see-the-attested-demand-letter-item-1"></a>**Ask the agency for a copy of the demand letter, embassy-attested if it is for more than one worker, and the contract** <sup>[21](#o21), [N53](#n53)</sup>. If it won't show them, don't pay, and complain to BMET (see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money)) <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-see-the-attested-demand-letter-item-2"></a>**Have them checked** at your DEMO or BMET (see [Before you pay anyone](#before-you-pay-anyone)) <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can workers check an attestation themselves on beriyadh.org?
> - What is the embassy's attestation fee?
> - What is the embassy's attestation processing time?

<a id="step-3-pass-the-skill-test"></a>

### 2.4 Step 3: Pass the skill test

The test is Saudi Arabia's Skills Verification Program (SVP), run by Takamol <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>.

**Who must take it:** every worker whose occupation is on Takamol's list; for other occupations it isn't required <sup>[11](#o11), [3](#o3)</sup>. A Pakistani government page counts 108 listed trades, including street, hospital, office and roof cleaners, load-and-unload workers and construction workers (see [Who qualifies](#who-qualifies)) <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>. Domestic workers don't need it <sup>[N15](#n15)</sup>. Check the occupation written on your visa against BMET's list of occupations by test centre (item 1): an occupation on it is tested in Bangladesh <sup>[62](#o62)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Where is the official, current list of occupations that need the SVP?
> - If your occupation isn't in BMET's centre list, does that mean you need no test, or that you can't take it in Bangladesh?

**To pass** you need real skill and work experience in the trade, and a score of at least 50% (item 8) <sup>[58](#o58), [N201 (2023)](#n201)</sup>. The test runs at 27 TTCs and BRTC Gazipur, the Bangladesh Road Transport Corporation's training institute <sup>[11](#o11), [63](#o63)</sup>. In August 2025 the count was 15 TTCs and BRTC training centres, equipped over the previous year under an agreement between BMET and Takamol <sup>[N92](#n92)</sup>. Bangladesh now certifies 60,000 workers a month in various occupations under the programme, the adviser, Asif Nazrul, said in January 2026 <sup>[N214](#n214)</sup>. In October 2026 recruiting agencies asked the expatriates' ministry for a simpler Takamol test process and lower fees <sup>[N33](#n33)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How many centres run the SVP test in November 2026: 27 TTCs and BRTC Gazipur, or 15?

You'll need <sup>[11](#o11), [N9](#n9)</sup>:

- your passport (valid);
- your NID;
- your own email address that you can open;
- a Visa or Mastercard endorsed for US dollars with USD 85 on it;
- the occupation on your visa.

1. <a id="step-3-pass-the-skill-test-item-1"></a>**Check which centre tests your trade** in BMET's [list of occupations by test centre](https://objectstorage.ap-dcc-gazipur-1.oraclecloud15.com/n/axvjbnqprylg/b/V2Ministry/o/office-bmet/2026/2/dc7e2b1f-5458-4a46-9197-958e2cd8941e.docx) ("TTC wise Occupation under SVP", on bmet.gov.bd under Training, Takamol); some centres test only loading and unloading, while Bangladesh-Korea TTC Dhaka tests 32 occupations <sup>[62](#o62)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-3-pass-the-skill-test-item-2"></a>**Register** on [svp-international.pacc.sa](https://svp-international.pacc.sa/) <sup>[11](#o11), [3](#o3)</sup>: press Sign-up, enter your names, NID, passport number and expiry, country, nationality, email, date of birth and a password (8+ characters with a capital, a small letter, a number and a symbol), all exactly as in your passport; tick "Agree to acknowledge that all the data are correct" and type the code emailed to you <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>. With only one name, write it as the first name and your father's name as the last; the same name in both counts as an error <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>. With a wrong passport number you can't sit the test, so fix it first through Support at the bottom right <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-3-pass-the-skill-test-item-3"></a>**Book:** sign in with your email, password and the emailed code; in Account Dashboard press Start Verification and choose your occupation (fixed after this), Direct Assessment (In-person), your division or city (the centre is assigned automatically), the test language and a green date; tick "I declare that the information entered is correct" and press Confirm <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-3-pass-the-skill-test-item-4"></a>**Pay USD 85** with the card's number, CVV, expiry date and holder name, then press Pay; the test ticket appears under Reservation and is emailed to you <sup>[N9](#n9), [3](#o3), [11](#o11)</sup>. Each retake costs the full fee again <sup>[N9](#n9)</sup>. Pay no one more than the fee: there is no way to pass for extra money, and a worker caught paying to pass has the account locked and can't go to Saudi Arabia <sup>[58](#o58), [64](#o64)</sup>. **If someone offers to fix the test for money,** report it to the police (call 999, or your local police station) <sup>[58](#o58), [65](#o65)</sup>, tell BMET's Takamol and Japan Support Centre (BMET Bhaban, 4th floor, Kakrail, Dhaka; 01901165981 or 01901165982) <sup>[64](#o64)</sup>, and complain to BMET (see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money)) <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-3-pass-the-skill-test-item-5"></a>**Prepare if you want:** a 3-day loading and unloading course costs Tk 2,000 at 24 TTCs, and its certificate isn't an SVP certificate <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>. Free courses <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>:

   - 5 days: gas station attendants (Kishoreganj TTC), sellers (Bangladesh-Korea TTC Chattogram);
   - 10 days: food and beverage counter staff (Dhaka Mohila TTC), barbers (Sylhet TTC), concrete finishers (Bangladesh-Korea TTC Dhaka), building and construction workers (Khulna, Jhalokathi, Moulvibazar, Nilphamari and Chapainawabganj TTCs).

6. <a id="step-3-pass-the-skill-test-item-6"></a>**Take the test** with your original passport, a photocopy, the ticket and one photo, arriving before the 30-minute briefing <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>. The computer test is 15 questions in 30 minutes in your chosen language; the practical test is 1 hour 30 minutes, in Bangla <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>. Phones and bags go in a locker; looking at another candidate counts as cheating <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>.
7. <a id="step-3-pass-the-skill-test-item-7"></a>**To change the date,** cancel and rebook once, up to 96 hours before <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>. Miss it and you are marked absent; you can pay and book again 96 hours after the slot <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>.
8. <a id="step-3-pass-the-skill-test-item-8"></a>**Get the result** on the SVP portal, where anyone can check a certificate is genuine <sup>[64](#o64)</sup>. A score of at least 50% passes, and the certificate is valid for five years <sup>[N201 (2023)](#n201)</sup>. Pakistan's SVP partner says a pass is uploaded within 24 hours; after a fail you book a new test <sup>[3](#o3)</sup>. To correct the certificate, press Support at the bottom right and raise a ticket with your name, email, a subject and description, Request Type "Suggestion", Client "Test Taker", your country and passport number, attaching your passport, ticket and certificate <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How do you pay without a dollar-endorsed card?
> - Does a card in someone else's name work?
> - What is the exact SVP page for checking a certificate?
> - How do you dispute a fail?

<a id="step-4-pass-the-wafid-medical-check"></a>

### 2.5 Step 4: Pass the Wafid medical check

Wafid is the Gulf Health Council's online booking for the health check everyone going to work in a Gulf country must pass; a medical centre in your city does the tests <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>. Agencies and brokers still call the check "GAMCA", after the system Wafid replaced <sup>[N215](#n215), [N118](#n118)</sup>. A result is valid for visa issuing for only 2 months, so take it when the agency is ready to file your visa application (the MOFA); agencies file only after a fit result, and one agency's order puts the medical first (see [If a relative or friend offers you a visa, item 5: Let the agency carry the file, in this order](#if-a-relative-or-friend-offers-you-a-visa-item-5)) <sup>[67 (2021)](#o67), [N120](#n120)</sup>. What it costs: see [Who pays what](#who-pays-what), and [Step 4: Pass the Wafid medical check](#step-4-pass-the-wafid-medical-check), items 1 and 2.

**You'll need** <sup>[38](#o38)</sup>:

- your passport (number, issue date, issue place, expiry date);
- your NID number;
- an email address;
- a phone that can receive a code;
- a card that pays online for USD 10 or USD 25.

1. <a id="step-4-pass-the-wafid-medical-check-item-1"></a>**Book the slip yourself** at [wafid.com](https://wafid.com/en/book-appointment/): press Book an Appointment, choose Bangladesh, your city and Saudi Arabia <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>; Standard (USD 10, centre assigned) or Premium (USD 25, you choose centre and date) <sup>[38](#o38)</sup>; enter your details exactly as in your passport, typing the passport number twice, choose Work Visa, enter the code sent to you and tick the box confirming the details are correct <sup>[38](#o38)</sup>. Pay by card (expiry as year then month) <sup>[66](#o66)</sup> and print the slip; to reprint it, open Print GCC slip and enter your passport number and nationality <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-4-pass-the-wafid-medical-check-item-2"></a>**Go to the centre straight away** with the slip <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>. It takes your photo and fingerprints at reception and again during the tests <sup>[N215](#n215)</sup>. You pay for the tests at the centre; the Council doesn't set that fee <sup>[N215](#n215)</sup>. Pay no more than about Tk 8,500-9,000 there <sup>[N118](#n118), [N120](#n120)</sup>; most workers pay at least Tk 17,000-20,000, as centres find "problems" to extract money <sup>[N123](#n123)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-4-pass-the-wafid-medical-check-item-3"></a>**Check the result** on [Wafid status search](https://wafid.com/en/medical-status-search/) with your passport number and nationality, or the slip number; it can take up to 26 days <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-4-pass-the-wafid-medical-check-item-4"></a>**If a detail is wrong,** ask the centre for a free change request and note its number and date <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>. If the report runs out before the visa is issued, you can book a new appointment 2 months after the report's date <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-4-pass-the-wafid-medical-check-item-5"></a>**If you paid but got no slip,** email support@wafid.com with <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>:

   - your nationality;
   - passport number;
   - full name;
   - email address;
   - payment date and time;
   - card number and cardholder's name;
   - a copy of the card;
   - a bank statement showing the payment.

<a id="dont-buy-a-fit-result"></a>

#### 2.5.1 Don't buy a "fit" result

Agencies and brokers sell fit reports for about Tk 20,000-30,000, but Saudi Arabia tests you again on arrival and sends a worker found unfit home at their own cost <sup>[N118](#n118), [67 (2021)](#o67), [23](#o23)</sup>. Some centres declare people unfit, then ask for a bribe to change it <sup>[N123](#n123)</sup>.

<a id="if-you-are-declared-unfit-or-overcharged"></a>

#### 2.5.2 If you are declared unfit or overcharged

**If you are declared unfit** (for example HIV, hepatitis B or C, tuberculosis, organ failure, uncontrolled blood pressure or diabetes, cancer) <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>, you can't retake the test for 3 months <sup>[N216](#n216)</sup>.

**If the result is wrong, the test was done badly or the centre demanded extra money:**

1. <a id="if-you-are-declared-unfit-or-overcharged-item-1"></a>**Complain in writing to the Gulf Health Council within 2 weeks of the test;** late or anonymous complaints are refused <sup>[67 (2021)](#o67)</sup>. Use the Complaint Form at the end of the [Wafid regulations](https://wafid.com/static/pdf/Regulations%20of%20Medical%20Examination%20of%20Expatriates%20Coming%20to%20GCC%20States%20for%20Work%20or%20Residence.pdf) <sup>[67 (2021)](#o67)</sup>, giving <sup>[67 (2021)](#o67)</sup>:

   - your contact details;
   - the centre's name and city;
   - what happened;
   - any proof (receipts, messages, another lab's report).

   Send it <sup>[67 (2021)](#o67), [68](#o68)</sup>:

   - to info@ghc.sa;
   - by post to P.O. Box 7431, Riyadh 11462, Saudi Arabia;
   - by hand to its office on Samha Street, Diplomatic Quarter, Riyadh (920020885).

   It says within 3 working days whether it accepts the complaint and decides within 30 days; the decision is final, and a centre at fault can be warned, re-checked, suspended or closed <sup>[67 (2021)](#o67)</sup>. Its Grievance Committee hears migrants' appeals <sup>[N216](#n216)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-you-are-declared-unfit-or-overcharged-item-2"></a>**In Bangladesh, complain about overcharging** to the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare's Enforcement-2 branch with your receipts, your full address and the centre's name and address (route: see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money), item 5) <sup>[N118](#n118), [69](#o69)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the official fee for the tests in Bangladesh?
> - How, and by when, do you appeal to the Grievance Committee?

<a id="step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre"></a>

### 2.6 Step 5: Give fingerprints at the Tasheer visa centre

Tasheer runs Saudi visa centres, and every work visa applicant gives fingerprints and a photo at one <sup>[N217 (2023)](#n217)</sup>. Dhaka: Jamuna Future Park (Ground Floor), KA-244, Kuril, Progoti Shoroni <sup>[N218 (2023)](#n218), [N217 (2023)](#n217)</sup>. Chattogram: World Trade Center Chittagong (2nd Floor), 102/103 Agrabad C/A, Commerce College Road <sup>[N219](#n219), [N217 (2023)](#n217)</sup>.

The agency usually books the appointment slip, which is free, so an agency asking money for it is overcharging <sup>[N120](#n120)</sup>; you can also book it yourself ([Step 5, item 1: Book free](#step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre-item-1)) <sup>[N217 (2023)](#n217), [N106](#n106)</sup>. At the centre you pay only Tasheer's service fee: about Tk 660-694, or about Tk 2,100-2,200 if put in the Lounge, its premium service <sup>[N120](#n120), [N9](#n9), [N106](#n106)</sup>.

**You'll need** (Tasheer's list for Pakistani applicants) <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>:

- your passport (valid 6+ months, 2 blank pages) and a copy;
- the Wafid report;
- the printed appointment letter.

1. <a id="step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre-item-1"></a>**Book free** at [vc.tasheer.com/appointment](https://vc.tasheer.com/appointment): Bangladesh, Appointment, Schedule, then visa type, centre, date and time <sup>[N217 (2023)](#n217), [N106](#n106)</sup>. One slot per person <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>. Pay no website or broker to book <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre-item-2"></a>**Change or cancel** in the Appointment menu up to the day before; a change within 3 days, or a no-show, blocks booking for 7 days <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre-item-3"></a>**Arrive 15 minutes early,** or the slot can be cancelled, moved to the paid Lounge or delayed 7 days <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre-item-4"></a>**At the centre** you get a token, pay the service fee, staff fill in the visa form on the Saudi Foreign Ministry's visa platform, still often called Enjaz, and you give biometrics <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>. Keep the original receipt to collect your passport <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre-item-5"></a>**Track it** on vc.tasheer.com and collect the passport within 30 days, or it goes back to the embassy <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>. The visa is electronic, with a QR code <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>; check it in [Step 7: Check the visa yourself](#step-7-check-the-visa-yourself).

> **Need to know**
> - What is Tasheer's own document list for Bangladeshi applicants?
> - Is Tasheer's own service fee for Bangladeshi applicants Tk 660 or Tk 694, and the Lounge fee Tk 2,100 or Tk 2,200?
> - What are its phone, email and complaint route in Bangladesh?

<a id="step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-start-before-step-5"></a>

### 2.7 Step 6: Give the agency your papers for the visa (start before Step 5)

Start this as soon as you have an agency, while you do Steps 3 and 4: the agency needs these papers to file your visa application (the MOFA) after your fit result and before your Tasheer visit ([Step 5: Give fingerprints at the Tasheer visa centre](#step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre)). Agencies ask for <sup>[N120](#n120), [N106](#n106), [N220](#n220)</sup>:

- a passport valid 6+ months with two blank pages;
- white-background photos;
- a police clearance certificate;
- the Wafid fit report;
- where your trade needs it, the skill test certificate.

Apply for police clearance first: it takes 7-15 working days, some agencies won't file the MOFA without it, and it is accepted for up to 6 months, so don't apply before you have a real job offer <sup>[N221](#n221), [N120](#n120)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the Saudi Embassy's own document list?
> - Does every trade need police clearance?
> - What is the card service charge for the police clearance fee?

**Get the police clearance certificate yourself** at [pcc.police.gov.bd](https://pcc.police.gov.bd/ords/f?p=500) <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>.

*You'll need:*

- a passport valid 3+ months <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>;
- a scan of its information page attested by a first-class gazetted officer <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>;
- if it shows no address, an attested NID, birth registration or ward councillor's certificate <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>;
- a mobile number;
- Tk 1,500 <sup>[19](#o19), [70](#o70)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-start-before-step-5-item-1"></a>**Register** with your mobile number and click Apply <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>. Forgot the password: SMS PCC PR and your mobile number to 26969 <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-start-before-step-5-item-2"></a>**Fill in the form** (personal details, then current and permanent addresses) and upload the scans; as your current address give the permanent or emergency address written in your passport, since the police check you at your current address, in that district or city <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>. Check everything on the last page before you submit: nothing can be changed after <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>. Keep the reference number <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-start-before-step-5-item-3"></a>**Pay Tk 1,500** by treasury challan at Bangladesh Bank or Sonali Bank (code 1-7301-0001-2681), or by card with a service charge <sup>[19](#o19), [70](#o70)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-start-before-step-5-item-4"></a>**Track it:** SMS PCC S and your reference number to 26969 <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>. It takes about 7-10 working days in Dhaka, 10-15 elsewhere <sup>[N221](#n221)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-start-before-step-5-item-5"></a>**Collect it** at Ready for Delivery <sup>[N221](#n221)</sup>: from the Metropolitan Police headquarters counter in a metropolitan city, or the Police Clearance Cell at the District Superintendent of Police's office elsewhere <sup>[N221](#n221)</sup>, with a copy of your passport or application; someone collecting for you needs your authorisation letter <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>.
6. <a id="step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-start-before-step-5-item-6"></a>**If delayed,** call 01320001824 or 01320001825 (Sunday to Thursday, 9 am to 5 pm) or email help.pcc@police.gov.bd <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>; DMP in Dhaka: 01320037339, 01320037329 <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>.

*Check it is real:* apply only on pcc.police.gov.bd, never through a middleman; forged certificates have been sold with QR codes leading to a fake site <sup>[N222](#n222)</sup>.

**If the job needs your education certificates,** apostille them on [MyGov](https://www.mygov.bd/services?category=sector&data=service-sector-67): you get an SMS and download the e-Apostille from your MyGov dashboard <sup>[N223](#n223), [72](#o72)</sup>. It costs Tk 200 per page online and takes up to 5 days; track it on [MyGov application status](https://www.mygov.bd/application-latest-status) <sup>[N224](#n224), [72](#o72)</sup>. If it isn't done in 5 working days, contact the Foreign Ministry's e-Apostille office, Segunbagicha, Dhaka (+8802-223381852, dgcnw@mofa.gov.bd) <sup>[72](#o72)</sup>, then complain on [grs.gov.bd](https://www.grs.gov.bd/), the government's complaint system <sup>[N224](#n224)</sup>. Saudi Arabia accepts apostilles without embassy attestation; anyone can check one by its QR code <sup>[N225 (2022)](#n225), [N223](#n223)</sup>. In January 2025 Bangladesh told Saudi officials that attestation of professional certificates at the Saudi embassy in Dhaka was delayed because the embassy had no cultural attaché, and they promised a quick solution <sup>[N59](#n59)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Do professional certificates still need attestation at the Saudi embassy in Dhaka, or does the apostille replace it?

<a id="pay-no-embassy-fee-or-visa-fee-to-anyone"></a>

#### 2.7.1 Pay no "embassy fee" or "visa fee" to anyone

The embassy charges nothing <sup>[N226 (2022)](#n226)</sup>, the employer pays the visa <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>, and Tasheer takes only its service fee <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>.

<a id="step-7-check-the-visa-yourself"></a>

### 2.8 Step 7: Check the visa yourself

Check the sponsor and occupation on your visa before you pay the rest and again before you fly (what a household sponsor means: see [Who the sponsor is](#who-the-sponsor-is); which occupations are free-visa signs: see [Avoid a "free visa"](#avoid-a-free-visa)). The Saudi labour ministry also cancels visas applied for on fake demand letters <sup>[N13](#n13)</sup>.

**You'll need:** the visa number (10 digits, different from the application number) or application number, and your passport number; before the agency files the MOFA, use the sponsor's Saudi ID, iqama or CR number instead (see [If a relative or friend offers you a visa, item 1: Check the visa and the sponsor before paying anything](#if-a-relative-or-friend-offers-you-a-visa-item-1)) <sup>[51](#o51), [N142 (2024)](#n142)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-7-check-the-visa-yourself-item-1"></a>**Online:** on [KSA Visa Track Application](https://ksavisa.sa/track-application), the Saudi Foreign Ministry's platform, enter the number, your passport number and the captcha, and press Track my application <sup>[51](#o51)</sup>. Press View to see your occupation, sponsor, visa type, validity and length of stay <sup>[N142 (2024)](#n142)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-7-check-the-visa-yourself-item-2"></a>**At BMET or your DEMO:** take copies of the visa and the passport's visa page to BMET at 89/2 Kakrail, Dhaka-1000, or your DEMO (addresses: see [Step 1: Find a real job](#step-1-find-a-real-job), While you look, item 1) <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>; BMET checks it online, free, within 3 working days <sup>[1](#o1), [21](#o21)</sup>. BMET also has a visa-check app on Google Play <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-7-check-the-visa-yourself-item-3"></a>**If the visa is fake, refused or cancelled,** use the complaint route (see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money)).

> **Need to know**
> - Does a refused work visa come with a reason or appeal?
> - What is the name of BMET's visa-check app?

<a id="step-8-orientation-bmet-clearance-and-the-flight"></a>

### 2.9 Step 8: Orientation, BMET clearance and the flight

<a id="do-the-compulsory-3-day-pre-departure-orientation-pdo"></a>

#### 2.9.1 Do the compulsory 3-day pre-departure orientation (PDO)

The orientation is compulsory, lasts 3 days and costs Tk 200 (Tk 100 plus a Tk 100 platform charge); get BMET clearance within 3 months of its certificate or do it again <sup>[21](#o21), [N121](#n121), [73 (2022)](#o73)</sup>. It covers migration, laws, work, safety and health abroad <sup>[21](#o21), [74 (2024)](#o74)</sup>.

**You'll need,**
at TTC Munshiganj <sup>[74 (2024)](#o74)</sup>:

- your OEP registration;
- copies of your passport and visa;
- proof of a bank account in your name (a cheque leaf or deposit slip copy) and one in a family member's.

1. <a id="do-the-compulsory-3-day-pre-departure-orientation-pdo-item-1"></a>**Enrol** at any TTC or Institute of Marine Technology <sup>[73 (2022)](#o73)</sup>: apply on OEP and download the [enrolment card](https://www.oep.gov.bd/pdo-enrollment-card) <sup>[21](#o21), [75](#o75)</sup>. In August 2025 the adviser, Asif Nazrul, said the interim government had shut the private Ami Probashi app and built its own online platform, on which BMET issues emigration clearance fully online <sup>[N227](#n227)</sup>. The app's route, if it still works: verify your passport, open PDO Training, choose a TTC and batch, pay Tk 100 plus a Tk 100 platform charge by bKash or Nagad, and download the admit card, which you show on the first day <sup>[N121](#n121)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does the Ami Probashi app still enrol workers in the PDO, and is the Tk 100 platform charge also taken on OEP?

2. <a id="do-the-compulsory-3-day-pre-departure-orientation-pdo-item-2"></a>**Attend all 3 days** (TTC Munshiganj: 9 am to 5 pm) <sup>[74 (2024)](#o74)</sup>.
3. <a id="do-the-compulsory-3-day-pre-departure-orientation-pdo-item-3"></a>**Download the certificate** from [OEP](https://www.oep.gov.bd/pdo-certificate) <sup>[75](#o75)</sup>; the centre's office corrects errors <sup>[74 (2024)](#o74)</sup>.
4. <a id="do-the-compulsory-3-day-pre-departure-orientation-pdo-item-4"></a>**Get BMET clearance within 3 months** of the certificate's date, or redo the orientation <sup>[73 (2022)](#o73)</sup>.

If anyone offers to get you through a course or clearance for money, call the police on 999, free and open 24 hours <sup>[21](#o21), [65](#o65)</sup>, or complain to BMET (see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money)) <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is BMET's own document list for the PDO?

<a id="get-bmet-clearance"></a>

#### 2.9.2 Get BMET clearance

BMET clearance is the emigration clearance, on a smart card <sup>[21](#o21), [1](#o1)</sup>. The clearance enrols you in the Welfare Board and its migrant insurance <sup>[20](#o20), [21](#o21)</sup>, and the card gets you the worker air fare (see [Before you fly](#before-you-fly)) and a PKB loan (see [If you need to borrow](#if-you-need-to-borrow)) <sup>[N205](#n205), [22](#o22)</sup>. A worker who leaves without it has to join the Board from Saudi Arabia to get its help (see [Welfare Board sign-up if you left without BMET clearance](#welfare-board-sign-up-if-you-left-without-bmet-clearance)) <sup>[76](#o76)</sup>.

**Use the agency.** For Saudi Arabia only recruiting agencies the Saudi embassy has approved can process visas and send workers, and once the okala is filed the visa sits in the agency's name, so the agency applies for the clearance <sup>[6](#o6), [N104 (2022)](#n104)</sup>. It takes 3 working days <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.

1. <a id="get-bmet-clearance-item-1"></a>**Do the orientation first** and get clearance within 3 months of its certificate (see [Do the compulsory 3-day pre-departure orientation](#do-the-compulsory-3-day-pre-departure-orientation-pdo)) <sup>[73 (2022)](#o73)</sup>.
2. <a id="get-bmet-clearance-item-2"></a>**Give BMET's own fingerprint,** the bio-enrolment ("bio finger"), for the clearance card; this is a second fingerprint after the Tasheer one, and the agency then gets the card <sup>[N120](#n120)</sup>.
3. <a id="get-bmet-clearance-item-3"></a>**Pay the fees:** the Wage Earners' Welfare Board's Tk 5,500 welfare fee and the one-time Tk 1,000 insurance premium (5 years, Tk 10 lakh on death) <sup>[20](#o20), [N228](#n228), [21](#o21)</sup>, and income tax: Tk 500 for a single visa; group visas Tk 1,200 skilled, Tk 800 unskilled <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>. The agency hands BMET the welfare fee as a pay order and the tax as a challan (list below) <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>. Agencies expect you to settle their bill before they take the clearance and buy the ticket <sup>[N120](#n120)</sup>.
4. <a id="get-bmet-clearance-item-4"></a>**Download the card** from [OEP](https://www.oep.gov.bd/employee-card) <sup>[75](#o75)</sup>.

The agency applies with these <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>:

- the visa copy;
- the contract;
- your attested registration card;
- its undertaking on Tk 300 stamp paper;
- the welfare fee pay order;
- the tax challan.

If it isn't done <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>:

1. Contact BMET's Director (Emigration), diremi@bmet.gov.bd, 01711-135901.
2. Then the Additional Director General (Employment), adgemp@bmet.gov.bd, 02-48321949; in Chattogram, 01913631797.

**Applying without an agency.** BMET's charter lets a worker with a visa he got himself apply alone <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>, but for a Saudi visa it isn't confirmed that BMET accepts this (see Need to know below), so don't plan on it. The charter's route is the emigration branch, 89/2 Kakrail, Dhaka-1000, or the Chattogram DEMO, Sarkari Karjo Bhaban-2, Agrabad (+8802333321639, democtg76@gmail.com), in 3 working days, with <sup>[1](#o1), [21](#o21), [77](#o77)</sup>:

- an application;
- the visa copy;
- any contract;
- your attested registration card;
- an affidavit on Tk 300 non-judicial stamp paper;
- the Welfare Board receipt.

> **Need to know**
> - Since September 2025, has BMET's emigration branch cleared a Saudi single visa for a worker who applied without an agency?
> - How does a worker applying alone pay the welfare fee and premium?
> - Is the Tk 250 clearance or smart card fee still charged <sup>[1](#o1), [N229 (2024)](#n229)</sup>?

<a id="before-you-fly"></a>

#### 2.9.3 Before you fly

1. <a id="before-you-fly-item-1"></a>**Fly before the entry visa expires:** an expired Saudi work visa is very hard to renew <sup>[N29](#n29)</sup>. In November 2026 Dhaka-Riyadh flights were suspended, and Riyadh-bound workers went via Jeddah or Madinah (see [Current situation](#current-situation)) <sup>[N7](#n7), [N29](#n29)</sup>.
2. <a id="before-you-fly-item-2"></a>**Check the ticket** under Manage My Booking on the airline's website with the PNR or e-ticket number and your last name; on Saudia it shows the booking if it exists <sup>[N230](#n230)</sup>. A seat held without your name and passport copy isn't a real booking <sup>[N231](#n231)</sup>; fares were about Tk 48,000-50,000 in May 2025 <sup>[N231](#n231)</sup>.

   - *The ticket racket:* ticket agencies said that under the previous Awami League government a ring formed in which Biman itself held back cheap tickets and released only expensive ones, and that this had not fully stopped by April 2025; trading in Saudi tickets began in late 2024 <sup>[N232](#n232)</sup>. "Group booking" schemes pushed Dhaka-Saudi fares up to Tk 1,90,000 in December 2024 and February 2025 <sup>[N233](#n233)</sup>.
   - *The booking rule:* the civil aviation ministry's directives of 11 February 2025 made the passenger's name, passport details and passport copy compulsory at booking; by March 2025, ATAB (the Association of Travel Agents of Bangladesh) said, fares had fallen to Tk 48,000-50,000, with some airlines selling Dammam and Riyadh tickets for Tk 35,000 <sup>[N233](#n233)</sup>. People in the sector said in April 2025 that ticket blocking had fallen a lot since the directives <sup>[N232](#n232)</sup>.
   - *Fares seen:* in April 2025 a direct Dhaka-Jeddah ticket for 1 May 2025 cost USD 552 (about Tk 67,000), against USD 307 (about Tk 37,500) from New Delhi to Jeddah the same day <sup>[N232](#n232)</sup>. In June 2026 the civil aviation minister said Biman's discounted "T-class" fares had cut Dhaka-Jeddah from Tk 62,000 to Tk 48,000 and Dhaka-Riyadh or Dammam from Tk 53,000 to Tk 48,000 <sup>[N234](#n234)</sup>.

3. <a id="before-you-fly-item-3"></a>**Leave one set of copies of your papers at home and carry at least 3** <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>. If papers are lost on the way, 999 can help: in March 2024 the bag holding the passport and air ticket of Momin Sheikh (45), a Saudi-based migrant from Sirajganj, fell from the Dhaka-bound Dhumketu Express; a fellow passenger called 999 saying he would miss his 2 pm flight without it, the Tangail railway police outpost found that a woman had picked it up, and the bag was handed back to him <sup>[N235 (2024)](#n235)</sup>.
4. <a id="before-you-fly-item-4"></a>**Open at least two bank accounts,** yours and a family member's <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>.
5. <a id="before-you-fly-item-5"></a>**Carry no packet for anyone** if you don't know what's inside (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>.
6. <a id="before-you-fly-item-6"></a>**Ask for the worker fare:** with a BMET smart card, a new worker gets a base fare of USD 360 plus taxes on Dhaka-Jeddah, Dhaka-Madinah and Dhaka-Riyadh flights <sup>[N205](#n205)</sup>. The civil aviation ministry's February 2025 directives require tickets to be sold at the Civil Aviation Authority of Bangladesh's approved fares and discounted tickets to be given to migrant workers, and Biman's managing director, Md Safiqur Rahman, said in April 2025 that discounted tickets were being given to workers going abroad <sup>[N232](#n232)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Is the USD 360 base fare still what a new worker pays in 2026?
   > - Where do you complain if an airline or agency refuses it?

<a id="scams"></a>

### 2.10 Scams

- **Free visa:** see [Avoid a "free visa"](#avoid-a-free-visa).
- **Shell-company visas:** visas in the names of companies that don't exist; the broker vanishes once the worker lands <sup>[N236 (2024)](#n236)</sup>. Check the company (see [Step 1: Find a real job](#step-1-find-a-real-job), Before you pay anyone).
- **Wrong visa or job:** brokers promise a 15-month iqama and send the worker on a 3-month tourist visa, after which the worker is illegal <sup>[N236 (2024)](#n236)</sup>; others promise one job and deliver another with a 3-month iqama <sup>[N236 (2024)](#n236)</sup>.
