2. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-2"></a>**Give notice** (how much: see [If you resign or are dismissed](#if-you-resign-or-are-dismissed)).
3. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-3"></a>**Get your final exit visa yourself** on Absher or Qiwa, without the employer's consent; the employer is notified, and you bear the consequences of any breach of contract <sup>[28](#o28)</sup>. Conditions: see ["If your employer reports you absent (huroob)"](#if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-huroob), item 2 <sup>[29](#o29)</sup>.
4. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-4"></a>**Collect what you are owed** within 1 week of the contract ending, or 2 if you ended it <sup>[50 (2023)](#o50)</sup>. End-of-service pay is half a month's last wage per year for the first 5 years and a full month per later year, part-years counted <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>; if you resign, you get a third after 2 to 5 years, two-thirds after 5 to 10, all after 10 <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>. Untaken leave is paid <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>.
5. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-5"></a>**Get your service certificate,** free, with your dates, job and last wage, and your papers back <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>. Or issue it yourself: qiwa.sa > Individual Account > Employment Certificates (Suggested Actions) > Apply Now > Service Certificate > select the job > Next Step > submit; it is approved at once for download <sup>[N282](#n282), [N296 (2024)](#n296)</sup>.
6. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-6"></a>**The employer pays your ticket home** <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>. Under Saudi labour law the employer or the Saudi government must bear a worker's cost of going home, and the labour ministry's spokesperson, Mike Goldstein, told the New York Times in 2025 that under no circumstances will a worker bear it; returned (Kenyan and Ugandan) workers and labour-rights groups said workers are in practice forced to pay, and those who cannot are sometimes held <sup>[N117](#n117)</sup>. If anything is unpaid, file friendly settlement within 12 months <sup>[97](#o97)</sup>.

<a id="work-visa-domestic-worker"></a>

## 3 Work visa: domestic worker

This visa is for work in a Saudi household, in one of 14 listed domestic jobs such as housemaid, private driver, nanny, cook or gardener <sup>[5 (2024)](#o5)</sup>. Domestic workers fall outside the Saudi Labour Law; the Domestic Workers Regulation sets their rights <sup>[30 (2024)](#o30), [118](#o118)</sup>. Musaned, the labour ministry's domestic-worker platform, handles contracts and pay <sup>[5 (2024)](#o5), [119](#o119)</sup>. The employer pays recruitment (up to SR 11,750 to the recruitment office for a Bangladeshi worker), the iqama, work permit and transfer fees <sup>[120 (2024)](#o120), [118](#o118)</sup>. A woman domestic worker pays nothing: her agency may take no migration cost from her <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>, and Saudi recruitment offices may take no commission from any domestic worker, before or after she starts work <sup>[5 (2024)](#o5)</sup>. No skill test is needed <sup>[N15](#n15)</sup>. You can change employer without consent only in listed cases (see [Work visa: domestic worker](#work-visa-domestic-worker), If something goes wrong) <sup>[30 (2024)](#o30)</sup>. Men going as house drivers: see [House drivers](#house-drivers).

> **Need to know**
> - What may an agency lawfully charge a man going as a house driver or other male domestic worker?

<a id="step-1-check-you-qualify"></a>

### 3.1 Step 1: Check you qualify

Most rules below are for women. Under the Saudi embassy's 2019 rules a man going as a house driver or other male domestic worker must be 21 to 60, and a driver needs a valid driving licence (see [House drivers](#house-drivers)) <sup>[10 (2019)](#o10), [118](#o118)</sup>.

- **Age.** A woman must be 25 to 45 for BMET clearance <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>; the housekeeping course (see [Step 3: Do the one-month housekeeping course (women)](#step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women)) takes women 25 to 45, returnees up to 50 <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>. Saudi law bans domestic workers under 21, and the contract normally ends at 60 <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>.
- **Children, health, education, consent** <sup>[121](#o121), [1](#o1)</sup>:
  - A woman whose youngest child is under 6, who is pregnant, or who has a contagious or incurable disease can't join the course <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>.
  - She needs class 5 (can be relaxed) and must read and write <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>.
  - She needs a no-objection letter from her legal guardian for the course and BMET clearance <sup>[121](#o121), [1](#o1)</sup>.
- **Never let anyone change your age.** Agents forge a second birth certificate to get a passport for an under-age girl <sup>[N297 (2020)](#n297)</sup>. Check the birth certificate the passport will use at [everify.bdris.gov.bd](https://everify.bdris.gov.bd/), the birth registration office's check page, with the 17-digit birth registration number and the real date of birth <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>. If an agent offers to change the age, call the Probashi call centre on 16135 <sup>[76](#o76)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Which office takes a report of a forged birth certificate used for a passport?

<a id="step-2-find-the-job-through-an-approved-agency"></a>

### 3.2 Step 2: Find the job through an approved agency

1. <a id="step-2-find-the-job-through-an-approved-agency-item-1"></a>**Use only an agency the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare has approved to send women domestic workers.** Approval requires a Tk 15 lakh security with BMET, a woman on the office staff, and the licence at stake if a worker is abused <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>.
   1. Ask for the ministry's approval letter (memo number and date) and check the agency's name and RL (recruiting licence) number are in it <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>.
   2. Check the licence is active on OEP (see [Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker), [Step 1, item 1: Ask licensed agencies what Saudi jobs they have, and check each one](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-1)).

   Complaints about agencies: sasenforcement@probashi.gov.bd, 02-41030255 <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does the ministry publish one current list of all agencies approved to send women domestic workers?
   > - Where does the ministry post the approval letters?
   > - How can a worker confirm an approval letter is genuine?

2. <a id="step-2-find-the-job-through-an-approved-agency-item-2"></a>**Pay nothing.** Any request to a woman for money for the visa, ticket, training or "processing" breaks the approval conditions <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>; complain as for a company worker (see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money)).
3. <a id="step-2-find-the-job-through-an-approved-agency-item-3"></a>**Get the job in writing before leaving:** contract terms, type of work, workplace, wage and rest periods <sup>[5 (2024)](#o5)</sup>; the wage is the one in the unified contract you both sign <sup>[5 (2024)](#o5)</sup>. The Arabic text is official and must be translated into a language you understand; get the Bangla translation before signing <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>. If the agency won't show the contract or its Bangla translation, or the terms differ from what you were told, don't sign, and complain as for a company worker (see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money)) <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How can a worker in Bangladesh see her Musaned contract herself before departure?

**How the Saudi side works.** A Saudi family picks a worker through a Saudi recruitment office on Musaned, which deals with the Bangladeshi agency; the visa goes to the Saudi embassy through the Foreign Ministry's platform <sup>[5 (2024)](#o5), [14](#o14)</sup>.

<a id="step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women"></a>

### 3.3 Step 3: Do the one-month housekeeping course (women)

The course is compulsory for women going as domestic workers and runs at BMET's Technical Training Centres (TTCs) <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>. Brokers sell course certificates; don't buy one <sup>[N298 (2018)](#n298)</sup>.

The length and the order have been in flux. BAIRA (the Bangladesh Association of International Recruiting Agencies, the agencies' trade body) said in January 2025 that the training for women going abroad had gone from one month to two <sup>[N299](#n299)</sup>. At a meeting on 30 January 2025 Bangladesh promised Saudi Arabia to shorten domestic-worker training while improving its quality <sup>[N59](#n59)</sup>. In May 2025 the expatriates' welfare adviser, Asif Nazrul, told Saudi Arabia's vice minister of human resources that women would finish the one-month training before getting the Saudi visa and be sent within two weeks of the visa after a short training <sup>[N78](#n78)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is the housekeeping course one month or two in 2026?
> - Must a woman finish the course before her Saudi visa is issued, and does a short training follow the visa?

1. <a id="step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women-item-1"></a>**Gather your papers;** bring the originals to the interview <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>:
   - your class 5 certificate (if any)
   - your guardian's no-objection letter
   - a photocopy of your birth certificate, NID or passport
2. <a id="step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women-item-2"></a>**Apply on OEP** ([employee.oep.gov.bd](https://employee.oep.gov.bd/employee/login-with-otp)) <sup>[56](#o56)</sup>:
   1. Log in with your mobile number and code.
   2. Open Training, then Trade/Skills.
   3. Choose division, district and centre, and press Search.
   4. Pick the housekeeping course, press Apply, then Process to Enroll and Confirm. "My Course" shows your applications.

   You pay the fee and choose the centre and start date online, from BMET's admission calendar <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is the housekeeping course fee?
   > - What is the course's exact name in the OEP list?

3. <a id="step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women-item-3"></a>**Interview** in person at the TTC: a committee checks your original papers and that you can read and write <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women-item-4"></a>**Live at the centre for one month** (24 working days), attending 90% of sessions; it covers cooking, appliances, language, culture, personal safety, reaching the police and embassy, and first aid <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women-item-5"></a>**Pass the final test:** 100 marks, pass mark 70%, 30 for language and 30 for practical work <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>.
6. <a id="step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women-item-6"></a>**Download the certificate** online; it is valid 2 years. If you haven't gone abroad within 2 to 3 years, do a 3-day refresher; after 3 years, the full course. A returnee with a year abroad does the refresher <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>.

<a id="step-4-medical-check-visa-and-bmet-clearance"></a>

### 3.4 Step 4: Medical check, visa and BMET clearance

1. <a id="step-4-medical-check-visa-and-bmet-clearance-item-1"></a>**Medical check, Tasheer fingerprints and checking the visa:** as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 4: Pass the Wafid medical check](#step-4-pass-the-wafid-medical-check) (the Wafid medical check), [Step 5: Give fingerprints at the Tasheer visa centre](#step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre) and [Step 7: Check the visa yourself](#step-7-check-the-visa-yourself).
2. <a id="step-4-medical-check-visa-and-bmet-clearance-item-2"></a>**BMET clearance** as for a company worker (see [Get BMET clearance](#get-bmet-clearance)), also bringing proof of age and the guardian's letter <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-4-medical-check-visa-and-bmet-clearance-item-3"></a>**Before you fly,** save the embassy labour wing's free line 8001000125 <sup>[N300](#n300), [79](#o79)</sup> and the Probashi call centre 16135 (+880 8000102030 from abroad) <sup>[76](#o76)</sup>, in your phone and on paper.

<a id="how-many-women-go"></a>

### 3.5 How many women go

Women going abroad for work fell from 1,05,466 in 2022 to 76,108 in 2023 and 61,158 in 2024, by BMET figures; Saudi Arabia took 66% of them in 2024, more than 40,000, and 17,786 of the 24,617 who went in January-May 2025 <sup>[N299](#n299), [N47](#n47)</sup>. BAIRA's (the Bangladesh Association of International Recruiting Agencies, the agencies' trade body) secretary general said in January 2025 that Saudi employers were no longer placing demand for Bangladeshi women domestic workers as before and were taking workers from African countries, and that bad publicity had cut women's interest in going <sup>[N299](#n299)</sup>. Bangladesh Nari Sramik Kendra (BNSK, a women migrant workers' organisation) said in June 2025 that Saudi domestic work pays little for the labour it demands and that Middle Eastern countries are taking workers from Africa instead <sup>[N47](#n47)</sup>.

<a id="scams-1"></a>

### 3.6 Scams

Age changes (see [Step 1: Check you qualify](#step-1-check-you-qualify)), bought certificates (see [Step 3: Do the one-month housekeeping course (women)](#step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women)) and fees taken from women (see [Step 2: Find the job through an approved agency](#step-2-find-the-job-through-an-approved-agency)) are covered at their steps <sup>[N297 (2020)](#n297), [N298 (2018)](#n298), [2](#o2)</sup>. Brokers also sell house-driver and domestic-worker visas from households that need no one; the worker arrives to no job, and working elsewhere is illegal <sup>[N236 (2024)](#n236), [118](#o118)</sup>.

<a id="your-rights-at-work-1"></a>

### 3.7 Your rights at work

- **Who arranges what:** the employer pays your medical test, iqama and renewal, and health insurance or treatment <sup>[5 (2024)](#o5)</sup>. The Saudi recruitment office must tell you the work and pay, and house and support you if you leave or refuse the employer <sup>[5 (2024)](#o5)</sup>.
- **Contract on Musaned:** you get an SMS link, enter your residence number and the code sent to you, and accept within 5 days; it is free (Musaned 920002866) <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.
- **Insurance:** a contract made through a recruitment office on Musaned is insured for its first two years <sup>[124 (2023)](#o124)</sup>. It covers:
  - permanent disability from an accident <sup>[124 (2023)](#o124)</sup>
  - your unpaid wages if the employer dies or is disabled <sup>[124 (2023)](#o124)</sup>
  - sending your body and belongings home if you die <sup>[124 (2023)](#o124)</sup>
- **Probation:** probation lasts up to 90 days; either side may end the contract <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>.
- **Hours:** you work at most 10 hours a day, with a half-hour break after every 5 hours, at least 8 hours' continuous rest, and one paid day off a week <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>.
- **Pay:** you are paid the contract wage monthly, into a bank account or digital wallet in your name linked to Musaned through your mobile and iqama numbers <sup>[5 (2024)](#o5), [119](#o119)</sup>. At most a quarter can be deducted, only for damage you caused, an advance or an official order <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>. The Saudi-Bangladesh recruitment agreement set SR 800 a month <sup>[125 (2015)](#o125)</sup>; that agreement was signed on 10 February 2015, and the SR 800 was about half the SR 1,500 minimum the Philippines agreed in May 2013, according to a Prothom Alo column <sup>[N275](#n275)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Is SR 800 a month still the agreed wage for a Bangladeshi domestic worker?

- **Housing, food and contact:** the employer must give you suitable housing and food or a food allowance <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>; the employer must let you contact your family and the embassy, and may not keep your passport, papers or belongings <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>. The expatriates' welfare minister told parliament on 5 April 2026 that Bangladesh's agreement with Saudi Arabia secures a woman domestic worker's right to keep a mobile phone, so she can alert her family, the embassy or the ministry at any time <sup>[N301](#n301)</sup>.
- **Leave:** you get up to 30 days' sick leave a year with a medical report, 15 on full pay, the rest on half <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>; after two years, if you renew, 30 days' paid leave and a return ticket <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>.
- **Only your own job:** the employer may not make you work for others or in another job <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>. If you work for anyone else yourself: up to SR 2,000 fine, a work ban or both <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>; working on your own account, SR 10,000 and deportation <sup>[5 (2024)](#o5)</sup>.
- **At the end:** you get your wages and dues within a week, or two if you end the contract, and end-of-service pay of one month's wage per four years <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>. Ending the contract without a valid reason costs two months' wages to the other side unless the contract says otherwise <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>; if you leave without one, you pay your ticket home <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>.

<a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1"></a>

### 3.8 If something goes wrong

**You can end the contract and keep your rights** if the employer breaks the contract, lied about the work, assaults you or behaves immorally towards you, gives you dangerous work or rents you out <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>. But don't just walk out (see If you are reported absent). Only your employer can issue your final exit visa <sup>[N145](#n145)</sup>.

**If you are reported absent (huroob).** Leaving without telling anyone can bring an absence (huroob) report, which makes you illegal <sup>[N302 (2024)](#n302), [30 (2024)](#o30)</sup>. The employer can withdraw it within 15 days of filing, after which it is final <sup>[30 (2024)](#o30)</sup>. The company-worker rules in "If your employer reports you absent (huroob)" (60 days to transfer, issuing your own final exit) are for company workers on Qiwa, not for you <sup>[N145](#n145)</sup>. If you must leave, go to the embassy or consulate (Complain in this order, item 2). Going home once the report is final: see [Going home without valid papers](#going-home-without-valid-papers).

**The labour ministry can move you to a new employer without your employer's consent** if <sup>[30 (2024)](#o30)</sup>:

- wages are unpaid for three months (in a row or not)
- nobody collects you from the recruitment office or shelter within 15 days
- no iqama is issued or renewed within 30 days
- you are rented out, given dangerous work or ill-treated
- the employer misses two dispute sessions
- the employer ends the contract during probation

Any other move: see Domestic worker to another household and Domestic worker to a company job.

**Complain in this order:**

1. <a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1-item-1"></a>**The embassy labour wing.** Call 8001000125 free in Saudi Arabia <sup>[N300](#n300), [79](#o79)</sup>, or use the form at [bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub](https://www.bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub/) <sup>[55](#o55)</sup>. *You'll need* <sup>[55](#o55)</sup>:
   - name, mobile and city
   - iqama or border number, and passport number
   - the problem type ("domestic worker problems" or "food, housing and pay problems")
   - a detailed description in Bangla or English
   - photos of your iqama and passport, if you have them

   For example: "I am a housemaid in [city]. My employer [name, phone] hasn't paid me for [N] months and keeps my passport. Please help me get my wages and go home." <sup>[55](#o55)</sup> Follow up on the "Your answer (SRS)" page ([bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub/index.php/main/viewrecord/](https://www.bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub/index.php/main/viewrecord/)) by reference, iqama or mobile number <sup>[126](#o126), [61](#o61)</sup>. Jeddah consulate labour wing: +966 12 689 6276 ext 131 <sup>[127](#o127)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1-item-2"></a>**If you must leave the house, go to the embassy or consulate,** not to a stranger: 8039 Dareen Street, Diplomatic Quarter, Riyadh <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. The labour wings run safe homes for women in Riyadh, Jeddah and Madinah, paid for by the Wage Earners' Welfare Board <sup>[103 (2024)](#o103), [76](#o76)</sup>; women in the deportation centre or embassy safe home have waited over a month, sometimes 3-6 months, to fly (2020) <sup>[N303 (2020)](#n303)</sup>. In September 2026 the state minister for expatriates' welfare said a safe home runs in Saudi Arabia <sup>[N116](#n116)</sup>, and in the same month parliament was told there are two safe homes in Saudi Arabia <sup>[N83](#n83)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How many safe homes do the labour wings run in Saudi Arabia now, and in which cities?
3. <a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1-item-3"></a>**The Saudi labour ministry.** File online through its "File a domestic labour lawsuit" service; the company-worker settlement service doesn't take domestic cases <sup>[128 (2024)](#o128), [97](#o97)</sup>. File within 12 months of the contract ending; the ministry tries to settle within 5 working days, then a committee or the labour court decides <sup>[118](#o118), [5 (2024)](#o5), [128 (2024)](#o128)</sup>. Musaned: 920002866 <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>. The labour wing helps settle disputes and attends court with workers <sup>[61](#o61)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is the link to the "File a domestic labour lawsuit" service?
   > - What are the steps to file through it?
   > - Does it work without Absher?

4. <a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1-item-4"></a>**From Bangladesh, a relative can act,** if the worker can't use a phone freely <sup>[N302 (2024)](#n302)</sup>; the labour wing can then trace the employer and recover wages <sup>[N304 (2022)](#n304)</sup>. The routes:
   - **A woman worker's case:** apply to the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare, Welfare-2 branch (aswelfare@probashi.gov.bd), describing what happened, with copies of her passport, BMET smart card and iqama, the agency's details and the accused person's mobile and email; it's free, and within 7 working days the ministry writes to the labour wing or BMET <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.
   - **To bring her home:** apply the same way with proof she is a migrant worker, her workplace and home address in Saudi Arabia, the agency's name and address and the employer's mobile <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.
   - **Unpaid wages of any worker, man or woman:** use the Welfare-1 branch route (see [If you aren't paid, item 4: From Bangladesh, a relative can ask the government to act](#if-you-arent-paid-item-4)) <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.
   - **If nothing happens:** write to the Joint Secretary (Mission and Welfare), 02-41030219, adsmission@probashi.gov.bd <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.
   - **A safe-home place:** a relative can request one, free, through the probashi kallyan (expatriates' welfare) branch at the Deputy Commissioner's office, the DEMO or the Welfare Board <sup>[76](#o76)</sup>.
   - **Advice:** Probashi call centre 16135 <sup>[76](#o76)</sup>.
5. <a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1-item-5"></a>**Legal help.** OKUP gives free legal advice and help filing cases: 01842773300 <sup>[N281](#n281)</sup>.

<a id="if-you-are-sexually-abused"></a>

#### 3.8.1 If you are sexually abused

1. <a id="if-you-are-sexually-abused-item-1"></a>**Get out to the embassy or consulate safe home** (Complain in this order, item 2).
2. <a id="if-you-are-sexually-abused-item-2"></a>**Report it** on 1919, the labour ministry's abuse-reporting line, which takes reports of physical, psychological or sexual abuse round the clock, in confidence <sup>[129](#o129)</sup>, and to the embassy labour wing on 8001000125 (Complain in this order, item 1).
3. <a id="if-you-are-sexually-abused-item-3"></a>**Know the risk if you are pregnant:** sex outside marriage is a crime there, and hospitals can report a pregnancy outside marriage to the police, even one from rape <sup>[N305 (2024)](#n305)</sup>. Ugandan domestic worker Farida Nassanga told the New York Times (2025) that her employer's husband raped her, and when she became pregnant the employer blamed her and put her on a flight home <sup>[N117](#n117)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does the 1919 line answer in Bangla or English?
> - How does the labour wing get a pregnant worker medical care and home?
> - How does a baby born there to a domestic worker get travel papers?

<a id="what-returning-women-report"></a>

#### 3.8.2 What returning women report

Officials of BRAC, OKUP (Ovibashi Karmi Unnayan Program, a migrant workers' organisation) and Bangladesh Nari Sramik Kendra (BNSK, a women migrant workers' organisation) said in January 2025 that women coming back from the Middle East complain of <sup>[N299](#n299)</sup>:

- being recruited on lies
- being made to work in more than one house
- 14 to 16 hours' work a day
- low and irregular pay, and irregular food
- physical and mental abuse, for some
- being detained by police and sent home after running from the kafil's (sponsor's) house, for some

Bangladesh Nari Sramik Kendra's executive director said in December 2024 that women workers in the Middle East cannot even leave the house <sup>[N115 (2024)](#n115)</sup>. BRAC's associate director Shariful Hasan said in December 2025 that brokers and even relatives often effectively sell women domestic workers under the name of sending them free, and that when they are abused abroad, the embassy's or authorities' intervention is limited, with no trial and no accountability <sup>[N98](#n98)</sup>. An OKUP study in 2025 found that 94% of women workers forced to return had suffered regular physical and mental abuse, 47% sexual harassment, and 82% had worked from dawn to midnight <sup>[N116](#n116)</sup>.

Bangladesh's foreign ministry told a parliamentary committee in October 2026 that 1-2% of Bangladeshi women workers in Saudi Arabia are abused, a figure it gets by asking women who come for passport services and from a third-party survey <sup>[N85](#n85)</sup>. It also reported that 1,364 women domestic workers had been rescued from Saudi Arabia and repatriated, without saying over what period; the committee chair called the number unacceptable <sup>[N85](#n85)</sup>.

**Cases:**

- **2019-2026, Rizia Begum** went in 2019 as a domestic worker, was abused by her employer and lost contact with her family after 2021, her family said; she came back in February 2026 unable to state her own identity at the airport, and was identified and reunited with her family with help from BRAC and the authorities (The Daily Star, cited in a Prothom Alo column, October 2026) <sup>[N83](#n83)</sup>.
- **October 2024, Firoza of Manikganj,** back after two years, said her pay was low but regular in the first year, was raised in the second but then not paid for 11 months in a row and paid all at once when she left; she also had problems with food and was beaten <sup>[N299](#n299)</sup>.
- **October 2024, Shahida Begum (40) of Faridpur,** back after two years three months in one household, said she wasn't fed regularly, was paid little, was sometimes beaten by the kafil's son, got no treatment for a sprained leg, and had SR 700 (Tk 17,500) cut from her pay for the return ticket the employer was supposed to provide <sup>[N299](#n299)</sup>.
- **December 2024, Shefali Begum of Narsingdi,** who went lured by a broker, said she was worked hard and not fed, and the householder beat her when she complained, broke her hand and didn't get it treated; back home she filed a case but, under local pressure, settled with the broker for Tk 30,000 and withdrew it <sup>[N115 (2024)](#n115)</sup>.
- **March 2025, Mary Nisimenta,** a Ugandan domestic worker, told the New York Times that after she jumped from a three-storey roof in Najran to escape her employer and was treated for a spinal injury, she reported the abuse to the police and the doctor, who told her to go back to work; when she refused, the Saudi placement agency sent her home <sup>[N117](#n117)</sup>.
- **2025, Sanjida Akhter of Narsingdi,** back after 2 months 25 days, said her employer made her work in his own house, his mother's and his in-laws', fed her only bread, and returned her to the Saudi recruiting agency's office when she fell ill, where the abuse began; she paid for her own ticket home and came back empty-handed <sup>[N116](#n116)</sup>.
- **March 2026, Anjana of Manikganj,** who had worked in Lebanon, Jordan, the UAE and Saudi Arabia since 2012, came back from Saudi Arabia on 20 March after 1 month 3 days, of which she worked 5 days and spent the rest being beaten at the Saudi recruiting agency's office; she paid her own ticket home with money from Bangladesh, and said the agency is paid for each woman it sends, so it sends women without a job <sup>[N116](#n116)</sup>.

<a id="house-drivers"></a>

### 3.9 House drivers

A house driver is a domestic worker in law, even if he was promised a company job: everything under Work visa: domestic worker applies to him, including working only for the household on the visa and depending on the employer for his exit visa (see [Work visa: domestic worker](#work-visa-domestic-worker), If something goes wrong) <sup>[5 (2024)](#o5), [7](#o7), [N54 (2020)](#n54), [118](#o118)</sup>.

- **Cost and pay.** Agencies have asked Tk 4-6 lakh for a house-driver visa <sup>[N133](#n133)</sup>, though the employer pays recruitment (see [Work visa: domestic worker](#work-visa-domestic-worker)) <sup>[118](#o118), [5 (2024)](#o5)</sup>. Get any payment agreed in writing and pay only into the agency's bank account (see [Before you pay anyone](#before-you-pay-anyone)). New private drivers earn about SR 1,500-1,800 a month <sup>[N133](#n133)</sup>.
- **Check the visa.** Brokers promise a company job and send the worker as a private driver, or never arrange the licence <sup>[N133](#n133)</sup>. Check the visa (see [Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker), [Step 7: Check the visa yourself](#step-7-check-the-visa-yourself)); it shows the occupation and the sponsor's name <sup>[51](#o51), [N142 (2024)](#n142)</sup>. The occupation must be private driver and the sponsor the household you were told about <sup>[5 (2024)](#o5), [N142 (2024)](#n142)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - How is "private driver" written in Arabic on the visa?

- **Licence.** The employer arranges and pays for your Saudi licence <sup>[5 (2024)](#o5)</sup>. With a Bangladeshi licence translated by an accredited agency, you can drive on it for at most 3 months <sup>[N306 (2023)](#n306)</sup>. For the Saudi licence <sup>[N307](#n307)</sup>:
  1. Book a driving school on Absher (Traffic Services, Book Driving School).
  2. Do the medical at an approved clinic.
  3. Open a file at the school; a beginner course costs about SR 2,500-3,000.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Is the official Saudi driving theory test offered in Bangla?

- **Crashes.** A fatal crash can mean blood money and prison (see [Driving](#driving)).

<a id="umrah-visa"></a>

## 4 Umrah visa

The Umrah visa is a pilgrimage visa for Muslims, bought only through a licensed agency (item 1 below) <sup>[N308](#n308), [N309](#n309)</sup>. It doesn't allow work or staying on for Hajj <sup>[130](#o130), [N310 (2024)](#n310)</sup>; for what working on it brings, see [Tourist visa](#tourist-visa), Can you work?

It is issued all year except the Hajj season (15 Dhul Qa'dah to 15 Dhul Hijjah) <sup>[130](#o130)</sup>. It costs USD 80 plus USD 10.50 visa service fee, USD 10.50 insurance service fee and the insurance; it is single entry, valid 90 days, with a stay of up to 90 days <sup>[130](#o130)</sup>. Packages cost about Tk 1.2 lakh to Tk 3 lakh or more <sup>[N311](#n311)</sup>.

1. <a id="umrah-visa-item-1"></a>**Choose a licensed agency.** Bangladeshis can't apply on Nusuk, the Saudi Umrah platform, themselves <sup>[N308](#n308), [130](#o130)</sup>. Pay only an agency on the Ministry of Religious Affairs' yearly list of licensed Hajj and Umrah agencies, on its website and [hajj.gov.bd](https://hajj.gov.bd/) <sup>[N309](#n309)</sup>. Fake agencies advertise on Facebook and YouTube <sup>[N309](#n309)</sup>.
2. <a id="umrah-visa-item-2"></a>**Give the agency your papers;** the agent books a Nusuk-approved hotel and transport <sup>[N308](#n308)</sup>. *You'll need* <sup>[N308](#n308)</sup>:
   - a passport valid 6 months with 2 blank pages
   - a white-background photo
   - a meningitis (ACWY) certificate from at least 10 days before arrival
   - a woman does not need a mahram (male guardian): Saudi Arabia's Minister of Hajj and Umrah announced in October 2022 that women can perform the pilgrimage without one <sup>[N312 (2022)](#n312)</sup>, although a December 2025 Prothom Alo guide still lists proof of the mahram relationship among the papers <sup>[N313](#n313)</sup>

   If a Saudi-based relative or acquaintance books your hotel instead, check it is approved by the Saudi government for Umrah pilgrims: many Makkah hotels are being cleared as the government rebuilds the city, the same guide says <sup>[N313](#n313)</sup>. The guide also says medicine is hard to get in Saudi Arabia without a prescription <sup>[N313](#n313)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Do Bangladeshi Umrah agencies still ask women for proof of a mahram relationship, and do they refuse women travelling without one?
3. <a id="umrah-visa-item-3"></a>**Enrol face and fingerprints** after applying, in Saudi Visa Bio, the Foreign Ministry's free app on Google Play and the App Store, with your email and a photo of your passport; anyone 6 or older, under-18s with an adult <sup>[131](#o131), [N308](#n308)</sup>.
4. <a id="umrah-visa-item-4"></a>**Travel within 30 days** of issue, or the visa is cancelled <sup>[N314](#n314)</sup>.

**Umrah visas used for trafficking.** According to the CID (the police Criminal Investigation Department), a ring promising jobs in Russia at Tk 2 to 2.5 lakh a month first sent 10 Bangladeshis to Saudi Arabia on Umrah visas, had them perform Umrah, then took them to Russia, where they were sold on and forced to fight against Ukraine; one, Humayun Kabir of Singra, Natore, was killed and another badly injured (February 2025) <sup>[N315](#n315)</sup>. A second group of 10 refused to go on once they learned recruits were forced to fight; their passports were taken, so they could neither work in Saudi Arabia nor go home, the CID said in February and again in June 2025 <sup>[N315](#n315), [N316](#n316)</sup>.

**Pilgrims hurt or stranded in October 2026.** Of the 24 Bangladeshis injured in the attack on Riyadh airport on 10 October 2026, 13 had come on Umrah visas, according to Foreign Ministry sources citing the embassy, which was in touch with the hospitals over their treatment <sup>[N25](#n25)</sup>. On 19 October 2026 the expatriates' welfare minister said many Umrah pilgrims were stranded in Saudi Arabia, many unable to return because of flight cancellations, and would be brought home when the situation normalised <sup>[N32](#n32)</sup>.

**If the agency cheats you,** write to the Secretary, Ministry of Religious Affairs, with what you paid, when and how, what wasn't delivered, and the agency's name, licence number and address; the ministry can order a refund within 7 working days or act against the agency <sup>[132](#o132)</sup>. Hajj and Umrah section: +880 2 55100899, morahajsection@gmail.com <sup>[132](#o132)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a refused Umrah visa be appealed, and how?
> - Who helps a pilgrim stranded in Saudi Arabia when the agency fails?

<a id="hajj-visa"></a>

## 5 Hajj visa

Hajj visas are issued only within Bangladesh's yearly quota, through the Ministry of Religious Affairs; the 2027 quota of 78,500 is full and registration has closed <sup>[N317](#n317)</sup>. Children under 15, people with serious chronic or infectious illness, and women late in or at high risk in pregnancy can't go <sup>[N318](#n318)</sup>. For Hajj 2025 the quota was 127,198, the Hajj agreement with Saudi Arabia was due on 13 January 2025, Hajj visas from 19 February and Hajj flights from 29 April <sup>[N319 (2024)](#n319)</sup>. Bangladesh is among the countries in Saudi Arabia's Route to Makkah Initiative, a Bangladesh foreign ministry official said in January 2025 <sup>[N71](#n71)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - When does pre-registration for the next Hajj open?
> - How is the opening of pre-registration announced?
> - What does the Route to Makkah Initiative change for a pilgrim leaving from Bangladesh?

1. <a id="hajj-visa-item-1"></a>**Pre-register:** Tk 30,000, with NID or passport (birth registration under 18) <sup>[133](#o133)</sup>.
   - Government package: on [hajj.gov.bd](https://hajj.gov.bd/) or at a DC office, Islamic Foundation office, Union Digital Centre or the Dhaka Hajj Office <sup>[133](#o133)</sup>.
   - Private package: through a licensed agency on the ministry's list <sup>[N309](#n309)</sup>.
2. <a id="hajj-visa-item-2"></a>**Primary registration:** deposit Tk 3.5 lakh <sup>[N318](#n318)</sup>.
   - Government package: at any Sonali Bank branch with a voucher <sup>[N318](#n318)</sup>.
   - Private package: into the agency's account shown on the Hajj portal <sup>[N318](#n318)</sup>. For Hajj 2025 the government's rule was that the whole package money be paid only into the agency's bank account or at the agency itself, the pilgrim keeping the receipt, with no dealings with middlemen, and women and children depositing together with their mahram (a close male guardian relative) <sup>[N319 (2024)](#n319)</sup>.
3. <a id="hajj-visa-item-3"></a>**Final registration:** pay the rest by the final-registration deadline the ministry sets; for Hajj 2027 it is 10 December 2026 <sup>[N318](#n318)</sup>. 2027 government packages cost Tk 5,05,648 to Tk 6,15,263, the private standard package Tk 5,13,648 <sup>[N320](#n320)</sup>. For comparison, 2025 government packages were Tk 4,78,242 (housing within 3 km of Masjid al-Haram) and Tk 5,75,680 (within 1.5 km) and the private general package Tk 4,83,156, against Tk 5,78,840 for the government general package and Tk 5,89,800 for the private general package in 2024 <sup>[N319 (2024)](#n319)</sup>. On 18 April 2026 the Prime Minister's spokesman said the cost of Hajj travel had been cut by Tk 12,000 per ticket <sup>[N321](#n321)</sup>. For Hajj 2025 each pilgrim paid the sacrifice (qurbani), SR 1,000, separately from the package; people aged 65 or over could go; and passports had to be valid to 31 December 2025 <sup>[N319 (2024)](#n319)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Is the sacrifice still paid on top of the package for Hajj 2027, and how much?
   > - To what date must passports be valid for Hajj 2027?
4. <a id="hajj-visa-item-4"></a>**Give the agency your passport and registration certificate, and do biometrics** in Saudi Visa Bio <sup>[133](#o133)</sup>.
5. <a id="hajj-visa-item-5"></a>**Collect passport, ticket, visa, ID card and luggage tags** before the flight <sup>[133](#o133)</sup>.

**Choosing a package.** Pilgrims who had performed Hajj several times said in November 2024 <sup>[N319 (2024)](#n319)</sup>:

- There are moallems (Saudi Hajj service providers) numbered 1 to 115 for South Asian countries, and how far your tent in Mina is from the Jamarat (the stoning site) depends on the price of the moallem cluster.
- Get in writing any extra service a moallem gives for an extra charge; these cause disputes in Mina.
- Buy an Islamic Development Bank (IDB) coupon for the sacrifice to avoid fraud over it.
- Ask in advance whether the package has a "fitra", a move between houses in Makkah, nearer or farther from the Kaaba, before or after Hajj.

**Scams.** The same pilgrims said a package cheaper than the announced price risks being cheated, that no agreement should be made with a middleman, broker or so-called moallem, and that pilgrims should check which agencies have complaints against them from the Saudi Hajj Ministry <sup>[N319 (2024)](#n319)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Where can a pilgrim see which Bangladeshi agencies have complaints against them from the Saudi Hajj Ministry?

Hajj on any other visa brings up to SR 20,000 fine, deportation and a ban of up to 10 years <sup>[N322](#n322)</sup>. For Hajj 2025, from 1 Zilqad (29 April) to 14 Zilhajj (10 June), Saudi Arabia barred anyone from entering or staying in Makkah and the holy sites without a permit to work at the holy sites, a Makkah-registered iqama or an official Hajj permit, and said visit-visa holders would be closely watched <sup>[N77](#n77)</sup>.

**Questions and complaints:** helpline 16136 (+880 9602666707 from abroad in office hours) <sup>[133](#o133)</sup>; against a private agency that took money and didn't deliver, complain as for an Umrah agency (see [Umrah visa](#umrah-visa), If the agency cheats you) <sup>[132](#o132)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the Hajj office's complaint route for services missing during Hajj?

<a id="family-residence"></a>

## 6 Family residence

A worker whose work permit is graded skilled (or high-skilled) can bring a spouse, sons under 25 and unmarried daughters to live in Saudi Arabia <sup>[94](#o94), [81](#o81)</sup>. Skilled means occupational groups 4 to 8 with pay above a ministry threshold and professional accreditation <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>. Elementary jobs (occupational group 9), and jobs in groups 1 to 8 paid below the skilled wage threshold, are graded basic <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>; whether basic-grade workers can bring family is not known (see the Need to know below). Each permit's grade shows in the employer's Qiwa account and can be corrected ([Bringing your family](#bringing-your-family)) <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>.

The family can get entry visas with the worker's work visa, finishing residence after arrival <sup>[94](#o94)</sup>. Besides the visa fee (item 4), each dependant costs SR 400 a month after the first 90 days <sup>[N323](#n323)</sup>. For relatives to come for up to 90 days instead, see [Family visit visa](#family-visit-visa).

> **Need to know**
> - Did the skilled-grade rule replace the SR 3,500 GOSI salary condition?
> - Do basic-level workers get family residence visas at all?

1. <a id="family-residence-item-1"></a>**Check you qualify:** a skilled or high-skilled grade on your permit <sup>[94](#o94), [81](#o81)</sup>; one guide also asks for a salary of at least SR 3,500 a month on your GOSI record (see the Need to know above) <sup>[N323](#n323)</sup>.
2. <a id="family-residence-item-2"></a>**Prepare the papers in Bangladesh,** attested and translated into Arabic <sup>[N323](#n323)</sup>:
   - the marriage certificate
   - children's birth certificates
   - your degree certificate

   An apostille replaces embassy legalisation between the two countries <sup>[134](#o134), [135](#o135)</sup>; get one on [mygov.bd](https://www.mygov.bd/) as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 6: Give the agency your papers for the visa (start before Step 5)](#step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-start-before-step-5) <sup>[N223](#n223)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Do Absher and the Saudi embassy accept an apostille instead of attestation for these papers?

3. <a id="family-residence-item-3"></a>**Apply on Absher:** My Services, Expatriates Services, New Family Visa Application, or at the recruitment (Istiqdam) office if Absher shows an error <sup>[N323](#n323)</sup>. Print and sign the form; your company's HR attests it on the Chamber of Commerce site within 30 days (SR 30) <sup>[N323](#n323)</sup>.
4. <a id="family-residence-item-4"></a>**Pay the SR 2,000 visa fee** through SADAD, the Saudi bill-payment system in your bank's app, with your iqama and application numbers <sup>[N323](#n323)</sup>; after 2-3 working days Absher shows the visa number; send it to your family <sup>[N323](#n323)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Is the SR 2,000 visa fee charged once per family or for each family member?

5. <a id="family-residence-item-5"></a>**The family applies through Tasheer** in Dhaka or Chattogram, which checks papers, takes biometrics and fees and sends the passports to the embassy <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>. Book as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 5: Give fingerprints at the Tasheer visa centre](#step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre) <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>, bring a medical report <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>, and track with Track Status on vc.tasheer.com <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What documents does Tasheer require for a family residence visa?
   > - What is Tasheer's fee for it?
   > - How long does Tasheer take to process it?
   > - Can a refused family residence visa be appealed?

A dependant can work in health or education through Ajeer (see [Family dependant to work](#family-dependant-to-work)) <sup>[N324 (2024)](#n324)</sup>. For a child born in Saudi Arabia, see If a baby is born in Saudi Arabia below.

<a id="if-a-baby-is-born-in-saudi-arabia"></a>

### 6.1 If a baby is born in Saudi Arabia

**Saudi registration:** register the baby on Absher (My Services, Services, Civil Affairs, Newborn Registration) within 30 days, or pay a fine <sup>[N325](#n325)</sup>.

**Nationality:** a child of a Bangladeshi parent is Bangladeshi <sup>[136](#o136)</sup>.

**Bangladeshi birth registration and passport:**

1. <a id="if-a-baby-is-born-in-saudi-arabia-item-1"></a>**Get the Saudi birth certificate** (Shahada Milad) <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-a-baby-is-born-in-saudi-arabia-item-2"></a>**Get a Bangladeshi birth registration** at the Jeddah consulate or at a parent's permanent address, with Saudi Arabia as place of birth <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-a-baby-is-born-in-saudi-arabia-item-3"></a>**Apply for the passport** at [passport.gov.bd](http://www.passport.gov.bd/) with <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>:
   - the Saudi birth certificate
   - copies of both parents' passports and iqamas
   - the Bangladeshi birth certificate
   - an A4 colour photo of the baby on grey or white
   - passport photos of the baby and a parent

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is the fee for the baby's Bangladeshi passport?
   > - How long does the passport take?
   > - Does the Riyadh embassy follow the same steps?

**Saudi residence:**

> **Need to know**
> - How does a parent add a newborn as a dependant on their iqama (residence permit), on Absher or at Jawazat, and what does it cost?
> - Does the baby need its Bangladeshi passport before the residence can be issued?
> - Does the monthly SR 400 dependant fee apply to a newborn?

<a id="family-visit-visa"></a>

## 7 Family visit visa

The family visit visa lets you visit a relative working in Saudi Arabia for up to 90 days; it is valid 90 days and costs USD 80 plus USD 21 service fees and insurance <sup>[95](#o95)</sup>. Your relative applies for it and the employer approves it <sup>[14](#o14)</sup>. It doesn't allow work and can't become a job ([Changing visa type](#changing-visa-type)) <sup>[N159 (2022)](#n159), [5 (2024)](#o5)</sup>. Bangladeshis without a US, UK or Schengen visit visa apply through a visa service centre <sup>[95](#o95)</sup>. Umrah is allowed, Hajj is not <sup>[95](#o95)</sup>. A visitor on a personal or family visit visa who disappears can be reported as absconded or missing by the host on Abshir Individuals, from 7 to 14 days after the visa expires <sup>[N131](#n131)</sup>. Around the Hajj season Saudi Arabia can suspend these visas and order holders out, on pain of fines and a ban <sup>[N326](#n326)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Will Saudi Arabia suspend family visit visas again before Hajj 2027?

1. <a id="family-visit-visa-item-1"></a>**Your relative applies** on [visa.mofa.gov.sa](https://visa.mofa.gov.sa/); the relative's work iqama needs at least 3-4 months left <sup>[N327](#n327)</sup>. The steps <sup>[14](#o14)</sup>:
   1. Log in with Nafath, the Saudi digital ID app.
   2. Choose Family visit visa and press Apply.
   3. Fill in the form, press Add and enter your details exactly as in your passport.
   4. Approve the agreement, Save and print.
2. <a id="family-visit-visa-item-2"></a>**The employer approves it** online ("Ratify individuals applications"), a domestic worker's employer too <sup>[14](#o14), [N327](#n327)</sup>; your relative sends you the visa document number <sup>[N327](#n327)</sup>.
3. <a id="family-visit-visa-item-3"></a>**You apply through Tasheer** (see [Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker), [Step 5: Give fingerprints at the Tasheer visa centre](#step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre)) with that number and proof of the relationship, such as a marriage or birth certificate, and track it with Track Status on vc.tasheer.com <sup>[N327](#n327), [N106](#n106)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What documents does Tasheer require for a family visit visa?
   > - What is Tasheer's fee for it?
   > - How long does Tasheer take to process it?
   > - Can a refused family visit visa be appealed?

<a id="tourist-visa"></a>

## 8 Tourist visa

Bangladeshis can get a tourist visa through Tasheer or as a package visa from an approved seller; the online e-visa needs a US, UK or Schengen visit visa <sup>[95](#o95), [138](#o138), [139](#o139), [140](#o140)</sup>. It is not a work visa (see [Can you work?](#can-you-work)).

**Through Tasheer:** the visa costs USD 80 plus USD 21 service fees and insurance, and lasts 90 days <sup>[95](#o95), [138](#o138)</sup>.

**Package visa:** this is an instant e-visa sold with confirmed flights and hotel by a travel agent approved by Saudi Arabia's Ministry of Tourism, open to Bangladeshis <sup>[139](#o139), [140](#o140)</sup>. It must include a round-trip flight and a licensed 4-star-plus hotel, at least SR 4,000 per adult for two days plus SR 1,000 a day after <sup>[140](#o140)</sup>. Buy only from a partner listed on [Visit Saudi](https://www.visitsaudi.com/en/campaigns/package-visa) <sup>[140](#o140)</sup>:

- Reserval ([reserval.com/packagevisa](https://www.reserval.com/packagevisa))
- Almosafer
- webook.com ([webook.com/en/e-visa](https://webook.com/en/e-visa))
- Almatar ([almatar.com](https://almatar.com/en/?visaType=tourist-visa))

1. Choose the package <sup>[140](#o140)</sup>.
2. Upload passport details and a photo <sup>[140](#o140)</sup>.
3. Pay; an automatic check follows <sup>[140](#o140)</sup>.

The visa is issued within 48 hours, valid 3 months, single entry, unchangeable after issue <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>.

**If refused:**

> **Need to know**
> - Can a refused tourist visa be appealed?
> - Who takes a complaint about a package-visa seller?

<a id="can-you-work"></a>

### 8.1 Can you work?

No. Working on a tourist or Umrah visa is illegal: a SR 10,000 fine and deportation the first time <sup>[5 (2024)](#o5)</sup>, and a re-entry ban of 3 to 10 years ([Going home without valid papers](#going-home-without-valid-papers)) <sup>[N151](#n151)</sup>.

Brokers sell 3-month tourist visas as jobs with a "15-month iqama", and the worker becomes illegal <sup>[N236 (2024)](#n236), [5 (2024)](#o5)</sup>.

<a id="business-visit-visa"></a>

## 9 Business visit visa

The business visit visa is for meetings, conferences and partnerships, not a permanent work contract; a "business visa" for a factory job is illegal work <sup>[141](#o141), [5 (2024)](#o5)</sup>. It costs USD 80 plus USD 10.50 visa service fee, USD 10.50 insurance service fee and the insurance; single entry lasts 90 days and multiple entry a year, with stays up to 90 days <sup>[141](#o141)</sup>.

1. <a id="business-visit-visa-item-1"></a>**Get the invitation letter** from the Saudi business <sup>[141](#o141)</sup>.
2. <a id="business-visit-visa-item-2"></a>**Apply through Tasheer** <sup>[N106](#n106)</sup>.
3. <a id="business-visit-visa-item-3"></a>**Get the visa** 1-2 working days after the letter and the application <sup>[141](#o141)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What documents does a business visit visa application need?
> - How is the invitation letter attested?
> - Can a refused business visit visa be appealed?

<a id="student-visa"></a>

## 10 Student visa

Applying on [studyinsaudi.sa](https://studyinsaudi.sa/), the Ministry of Education's platform, is free; agents who charge to apply are not authorised <sup>[N328](#n328)</sup>. Official Saudi sites end in .sa or gov.sa <sup>[142](#o142)</sup>. Full scholarships have covered tuition, airfare, housing, meals, books and a stipend <sup>[143](#o143), [N329](#n329)</sup>; a June 2024 Prothom Alo guide put the stipend at about SR 840 a month, with free housing and medical care, a yearly return ticket home and a preparation allowance, and said some universities give married students a family visa and quarters <sup>[N330 (2024)](#n330)</sup>. Universities that take foreign students include the Islamic University of Madinah, King Abdulaziz University in Jeddah, Umm Al-Qura University in Makkah and Taibah University in Madinah <sup>[N330 (2024)](#n330)</sup>. King Fahd University, a public university in Dhahran, offered Bangladeshis full scholarships for master's and PhD study in 2024, free to apply, covering tuition, a monthly stipend, housing, health insurance, textbooks, flights and cafeteria meals <sup>[N331 (2024)](#n331)</sup>. It asked for a four-year bachelor's for a master's or a two-year master's for a PhD, and IELTS or TOEFL, transcripts, three reference letters, a CV, a photo, a research proposal and a one-page statement of purpose; the 2024 deadline was 29 June <sup>[N331 (2024)](#n331)</sup>. Applications are taken only in windows; the last one closed on 6 October 2026 <sup>[N328](#n328)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - When does the next application window open?

1. <a id="student-visa-item-1"></a>**Check you qualify** <sup>[N328](#n328)</sup>:
   - ages 17 to 25 for a bachelor's, up to 30 for a master's, 35 for a PhD
   - IELTS 5.5 or English-medium schooling for courses in English

   The June 2024 Prothom Alo guide gives different rules <sup>[N330 (2024)](#n330)</sup>:
   - ages 17 to 23 (25 at some universities), with at least GPA 4 at HSC or Alim, though some universities state no grade or accept less
   - no direct entry to a bachelor's: you first apply for the four-level Diploma in Arabic at the university's Arabic language institute, which takes two years (one to one and a half if you start at level two or three), and enter the degree only with satisfactory results; Arabic and Islamic subjects are taught wholly in Arabic, others in English
   - a woman needs a copy of her mahram's (male guardian relative's) iqama, because Saudi rules didn't allow women higher education without a mahram

   > **Need to know**
   > - Is the bachelor's age limit 23 or 25?
   > - Must a Bangladeshi first pass the Arabic diploma, or can a student with IELTS 5.5 enter a degree taught in English directly?
   > - Does a woman still need a mahram's iqama to apply?

2. <a id="student-visa-item-2"></a>**Apply** to up to 5 universities, 3 programmes each <sup>[N328](#n328)</sup>, uploading <sup>[142](#o142)</sup>:
   - a valid passport
   - a medical report showing no infectious disease
   - attested certificates
   - whatever the university asks

   Only contact details can be changed after submitting <sup>[142](#o142)</sup>. The June 2024 guide described the papers as PDFs of at most 200 KB: a passport valid at least two years; a white-background photo without cap or glasses; a medical fitness certificate from the civil surgeon or any medical centre; a character certificate from the Islamic Foundation, two known scholars or a college teacher; and HSC or Alim certificates and mark sheets translated into Arabic at an approved centre, notarised, then attested by the Islamic Foundation, the education board, the education and foreign ministries and finally the Saudi embassy in Bangladesh <sup>[N330 (2024)](#n330)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does an apostille now replace the attestation chain for study certificates, as it does for work papers?
3. <a id="student-visa-item-3"></a>**Wait for the decision,** which comes only through the platform: the university approves, then it and the ministry complete admission; a rejection means a requirement wasn't met <sup>[142](#o142)</sup>. Help: a support ticket on the platform <sup>[142](#o142)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How can a student confirm an admission letter directly with the university?

4. <a id="student-visa-item-4"></a>**Get the study visa,** renewed yearly <sup>[143](#o143)</sup>. It needs <sup>[143](#o143)</sup>:
   - admission from an accredited institution
   - proof of funds or a scholarship
   - a medical report
   - medical insurance
   - proof of the language of study

   > **Need to know**
   > - Where does a Bangladeshi student get the study visa issued: Tasheer or the embassy?
   > - What is the study visa fee?
   > - How long does processing take?
   > - Can a refused study visa be appealed?

<a id="other-visas"></a>

## 11 Other visas

<a id="temporary-work-visa"></a>

### 11.1 Temporary work visa

A Saudi employer requests it on Qiwa (the labour ministry's work platform) for project or contract work; it lasts 90 days plus one 90-day extension, gives no iqama, and the employer pays its SR 1,000 fee <sup>[N332](#n332)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Who finds the job for a Bangladeshi on a temporary work visa?
> - Are a recruiting agency and BMET clearance involved?
> - What does the worker pay?
> - What scams run on this route?
> - Can a temporary work visa be converted to an iqama?

<a id="hajj-seasonal-work-visa"></a>

### 11.2 Hajj seasonal work visa

This visa is for Hajj-season work only and can't be renewed or converted to a work visa; whether Bangladeshis can get it at all is not known. Entry is from 1 Shawwal for a 4-month stay, and the entry fee is SR 1,000 <sup>[N333 (2023)](#n333)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Do Bangladeshis get the Hajj seasonal work visa?
> - Through whom do they get it?

<a id="stopover-transit-visa"></a>

### 11.3 Stopover (transit) visa

This visa is for Saudia passengers flying through Saudi Arabia, open to Bangladeshis <sup>[N334](#n334), [N335 (2024)](#n335)</sup>. It allows one stay of up to 96 hours, with no extension; passport control decides entry <sup>[N334](#n334)</sup>.

*You'll need* <sup>[N334](#n334)</sup>:

- a passport valid 6 months
- a confirmed onward ticket
- a visa for the final destination
- a 200 x 200 pixel photo of at most 20 KB

1. <a id="stopover-transit-visa-item-1"></a>**Apply on saudia.com while booking** (Seats & Extras or Additional Services) <sup>[N334](#n334)</sup>.
2. <a id="stopover-transit-visa-item-2"></a>**Pay with the flight** <sup>[N334](#n334)</sup>.
3. <a id="stopover-transit-visa-item-3"></a>**The visa and insurance come by email** <sup>[N334](#n334)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the stopover visa fee?
> - Is any work allowed on a stopover visa?

<a id="premium-residency"></a>

### 11.4 Premium Residency

Premium Residency is residence not tied to an employer, sold by the Premium Residency Center at pr.gov.sa; its products are for people of exceptional competence or talent, business investors, entrepreneurs and property owners, plus a limited- or unlimited-duration residence <sup>[144](#o144)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What does Premium Residency cost?
> - Who qualifies for Premium Residency?

<a id="medical-treatment-visa"></a>

### 11.5 Medical treatment visa

The medical treatment visa, for treatment in Saudi Arabia, costs USD 80 plus two USD 10.50 digital-service fees and the insurance, priced by the insurer you choose; a single-entry visa lasts 90 days and a multiple-entry one a year, with stays up to 90 days; processing takes a minute to 3 working days; it doesn't allow Hajj <sup>[145](#o145)</sup>.

Bangladeshis without a US, UK or Schengen visa, or US, UK, EU or Gulf residence, apply in two steps <sup>[145](#o145)</sup>:

1. <a id="medical-treatment-visa-item-1"></a>**Apply on ksavisa.sa** <sup>[145](#o145)</sup>.
2. <a id="medical-treatment-visa-item-2"></a>**Then go through a visa service centre or the Saudi embassy** <sup>[145](#o145)</sup>. Tasheer runs the visa centre in Dhaka (see [Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker), [Step 5: Give fingerprints at the Tasheer visa centre](#step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre)) <sup>[N218 (2023)](#n218), [N217 (2023)](#n217)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What documents does the medical treatment visa need?
> - Does Tasheer's Dhaka centre take medical visa applications?

<a id="changing-visa-type"></a>

## 12 Changing visa type

**Company worker to another company:** see [If you want to change employer](#if-you-want-to-change-employer).

<a id="visit-tourist-umrah-or-business-visa-to-work"></a>

### 12.1 Visit, tourist, Umrah or business visa to work

**Family visit, tourist and business visit visas:** all three are visit visas <sup>[95](#o95), [138](#o138), [141](#o141)</sup>, and a visit visa can't be changed to an iqama; anyone charging to "convert" one is lying <sup>[N159 (2022)](#n159)</sup>. Family visitors may not work at all <sup>[N159 (2022)](#n159)</sup>.

**Umrah visa:** it may not be used for work <sup>[N310 (2024)](#n310)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can an Umrah visa be changed to a work visa or iqama?

For what working on a tourist or Umrah visa brings, see [Tourist visa](#tourist-visa), Can you work?

**Hajj seasonal work visa:** it can't be renewed or converted to a work visa <sup>[N333 (2023)](#n333)</sup> ([Other visas](#other-visas), Hajj seasonal work visa).

**Temporary work visa:** it gives no iqama <sup>[N332](#n332)</sup>; whether it can be converted is a Need to know under Other visas, Temporary work visa.

<a id="family-dependant-to-work"></a>

### 12.2 Family dependant to work

A spouse or mahram (a close male guardian relative) on a dependant iqama can work for a health or education employer, staying on the family sponsorship, through Ajeer ([ajeer.qiwa.sa](https://ajeer.qiwa.sa)) <sup>[32 (2024)](#o32), [146 (2024)](#o146)</sup>.

1. <a id="family-dependant-to-work-item-1"></a>**You'll need** <sup>[32 (2024)](#o32), [N324 (2024)](#n324)</sup>:
   - valid iqamas for you and the main worker;
   - age 18 or over;
   - any licence the job requires;
   - an offer from a health or education employer rated low green or higher on Nitaqat, the Saudisation rating.
2. <a id="family-dependant-to-work-item-2"></a>**The employer issues the contract on Ajeer** (Contracts, Issue Contract, Contracting Service Between Workforce, Ajeer Accompanying Service); once approved, Ajeer issues your work permit, free <sup>[32 (2024)](#o32), [N324 (2024)](#n324)</sup>. If it stalls, call Ajeer on 920011040 or the labour ministry on 19911 <sup>[146 (2024)](#o146), [102 (2024)](#o102)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How long does Ajeer take to issue the work permit after the employer issues the contract?

> **Need to know**
> - Can a dependant on a family iqama work for an employer outside health and education?
> - If so, through which platform or procedure does the dependant get the work permit?

<a id="domestic-worker-to-another-household"></a>

### 12.3 Domestic worker to another household

A transfer is done on Musaned ([musaned.gov.sa](https://musaned.gov.sa)) <sup>[32 (2024)](#o32)</sup>. You pay nothing: the service is free and the employer bears transfer fees; payments between employers go through Musaned, capped at SR 16,188 for a Bangladeshi worker <sup>[32 (2024)](#o32), [118](#o118), [N336 (2023)](#n336)</sup>.

1. <a id="domestic-worker-to-another-household-item-1"></a>**You'll need** <sup>[N336 (2023)](#n336), [32 (2024)](#o32)</sup>:
   - the agreement of your current employer, you and the new employer;
   - your own Absher account.
2. <a id="domestic-worker-to-another-household-item-2"></a>**Your employer starts the transfer on Musaned** <sup>[32 (2024)](#o32)</sup>.
3. <a id="domestic-worker-to-another-household-item-3"></a>**The new employer accepts or rejects it there** <sup>[32 (2024)](#o32)</sup>.
4. <a id="domestic-worker-to-another-household-item-4"></a>**A new contract is drawn up and the request completed through Absher** <sup>[32 (2024)](#o32)</sup>. You get a new fixed-period work contract <sup>[N336 (2023)](#n336)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How long does a Musaned transfer to another household take?
   > - How can the worker see the new contract's terms before agreeing to the transfer?

**Without your employer's consent,** only the ministry can move you, and only in the listed cases, such as 3 months' unpaid wages or no iqama within 30 days ([Work visa: domestic worker](#work-visa-domestic-worker), If something goes wrong) <sup>[30 (2024)](#o30)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How does a domestic worker ask the ministry for a transfer in one of these cases, and how long does it take?

**If your employer refuses or there is a money dispute,** file a free claim with the domestic workers' dispute committees on the ministry's Individuals Portal or unified app (My Services, Settlement Claims for Domestic Workers) with your original iqama or passport <sup>[32 (2024)](#o32)</sup>. Musaned: 920002866 <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>; embassy labour wing, free: 8001000125 <sup>[79](#o79)</sup>.

<a id="domestic-worker-to-a-company-job"></a>

### 12.4 Domestic worker to a company job

Yes, but only in your first year: after your iqama is renewed the request is refused <sup>[N293 (2022)](#n293)</sup>.

1. <a id="domestic-worker-to-a-company-job-item-1"></a>**You'll need** <sup>[N293 (2022)](#n293)</sup>:
   - a company that has signed a work contract with you;
   - your mobile number, to which Qiwa sends the request.
2. <a id="domestic-worker-to-a-company-job-item-2"></a>**The company requests the transfer on Qiwa,** naming your new job <sup>[N293 (2022)](#n293)</sup>.
3. <a id="domestic-worker-to-a-company-job-item-3"></a>**Qiwa sends you an SMS;** approve or reject it within 10 days <sup>[N293 (2022)](#n293)</sup>.
4. <a id="domestic-worker-to-a-company-job-item-4"></a>**Your household employer is then asked to agree** <sup>[N293 (2022)](#n293)</sup>. Qiwa support: 920000105 <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What happens to the transfer request if the household employer refuses?
   > - What does a transfer from a household to a company cost, and who pays it?
   > - How long does a transfer from a household to a company take?

<a id="changing-the-job-title-on-the-permit"></a>

### 12.5 Changing the job title on the permit

Only an employer in the platinum or green Nitaqat band can change it, on Qiwa (Change expatriate profession); it is free and takes effect at once, but a job reserved for Saudis can't be given to a foreign worker <sup>[147](#o147), [35](#o35)</sup>. Since January 2026, titles including sales representative, marketing specialist and procurement manager can't be given to foreign workers this way <sup>[N337](#n337)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What can a worker do if the employer refuses to change the job title on the iqama?

<a id="asylum"></a>

## 13 Asylum

Saudi Arabia has no asylum system, hasn't signed the 1951 Refugee Convention and generally doesn't grant asylum <sup>[N338](#n338)</sup>. People there illegally, including pilgrimage overstayers, are refused refugee status and deported <sup>[N338](#n338)</sup>. The only protection is UNHCR, the UN refugee agency: people it recognises may stay temporarily, with valid identity papers, until resettled or returned; those without travel documents are sometimes detained indefinitely <sup>[N338](#n338)</sup>.

Apply by email to saprstaf@unhcr.org with <sup>[148](#o148)</sup>:

- your full name;
- family members' names;
- date of birth, sex and nationality;
- address and phone;
- any earlier UNHCR registration;
- the reason.

Toll-free line 009668001000281, Sunday, Tuesday and Thursday <sup>[148](#o148)</sup>. The Riyadh office, in the Diplomatic Quarter, is by appointment only <sup>[148](#o148)</sup>.

<a id="sending-money-home"></a>

## 14 Sending money home

**Use a legal channel,** never hundi (the illegal informal channel) or unlicensed agents <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>. Saudi police have arrested people for sending money outside legal channels, and Bangladesh has frozen bKash, Nagad and Rocket accounts linked to hundi <sup>[N339 (2021)](#n339), [N340 (2023)](#n340)</sup>. Without a valid iqama you can't use a bank or send money formally <sup>[23](#o23)</sup>. Saudi Arabia's ambassador in Dhaka said in August 2024 that about 32 lakh Bangladeshis in Saudi Arabia send about USD 5 billion a year through legal channels and another USD 5 billion through informal ones <sup>[N341 (2024)](#n341)</sup>.

**The numbers.** Saudi Arabia was the largest source of Bangladesh's remittances in 2025, 15.52% of USD 32.82 billion, according to RMMRU (the Refugee and Migratory Movements Research Unit), and Bangladesh Bank's governor called it the biggest source of remittances and foreign currency in October 2025 <sup>[N66](#n66), [N79](#n79)</sup>. By Bangladesh Bank figures it had fallen from USD 4.54 billion in 2021-22, when it was first, to USD 3.76 billion in 2022-23, and then by USD 1.03 billion, about 27%, in 2023-24, to fourth place <sup>[N342 (2024)](#n342), [N70 (2024)](#n70)</sup>. In March 2024 RMMRU's founding chair Tasneem Siddiqui put the fall down to many sending through hundi, over the gap in exchange rates and distrust of banks, and to many workers who had recently gone on legal visas not getting jobs <sup>[N342 (2024)](#n342)</sup>. In March 2025 the governor said not all Saudi remittances come straight to Bangladesh: a dishonest ring buys them in Saudi Arabia, routes them through Dubai, hoards them and sells them to Bangladeshi banks at a higher price <sup>[N343](#n343)</sup>.

**Compare costs** on the World Bank's price comparison, [remittanceprices.worldbank.org/corridor/SA/BD](https://remittanceprices.worldbank.org/corridor/SA/BD). Sending SR 750 cost <sup>[149](#o149)</sup>:

- about 7-9% through STC Pay, TeleMoney, Western Union or MoneyGram; STC Pay delivers in under an hour to cash pickup, a bank account or a mobile wallet;
- about 15-16% at bank branches, which charge SR 75.

Bangladesh Bank's governor said in October 2025 that expatriates in Saudi Arabia pay 6 to 10% to send money, comparatively much higher and a huge burden on them <sup>[N79](#n79)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What documents do STC Pay, TeleMoney and the other remittance apps need to open an account?

**To a bKash account:**

1. <a id="sending-money-home-item-1"></a>**You'll need** <sup>[N344](#n344)</sup>:
   - the family member's bKash number;
   - their full name exactly as registered when the account was opened;
   - the cash or money in your app.
2. <a id="sending-money-home-item-2"></a>**Use a bKash partner:** Saudi Awwal Bank, STC Pay, URpay, Mobily Pay, tiqmo, barq, Western Union or MoneyGram <sup>[N344](#n344)</sup>.
3. <a id="sending-money-home-item-3"></a>**Give the number and name with the cash, or enter them in its app** <sup>[N344](#n344)</sup>.

The limit is Tk 2.5 lakh per transfer, as many times as you like <sup>[N344](#n344)</sup>. Your family gets the money plus the 2.5% government incentive automatically, and can cash out at BRAC Bank, City Bank and Q-Cash ATMs for Tk 7 per thousand <sup>[N345](#n345), [N346 (2022)](#n346), [N344](#n344)</sup>. A banker's column in April 2025 also gave the incentive as 2.5% <sup>[N75](#n75)</sup>, but the expatriates' ministry said in June 2025 that the government continues a 2% incentive on remittances sent through banks <sup>[N91](#n91)</sup>. In March 2024 many banks added their own incentive equal to the government's, so a legal transfer fetched over Tk 115 a dollar against an official rate of Tk 110, and some banks short of dollars paid over Tk 120 <sup>[N342 (2024)](#n342)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is the government's remittance incentive 2% or 2.5% now?
> - Which banks still add their own incentive on top?

**Sending to a brother, sister or in-law.** Many workers in Saudi Arabia and the Gulf send money to brothers', sisters', in-laws' and friends' bank accounts, because many elderly parents in villages have no bank account, children are minors and wives don't open accounts <sup>[N61 (2024)](#n61)</sup>. A June 2024 budget proposal would tax cash and gifts, remittances included, received from anyone other than a spouse, child or parent; an NBR (National Board of Revenue) official said tax could be avoided only if the sender shows the money as his own and the recipient shows it as a liability in the tax return, but the NBR gave no formal explanation <sup>[N61 (2024)](#n61)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Was the June 2024 tax on remittances received from relatives other than a spouse, child or parent enacted, and does it apply now?

**If money doesn't arrive or goes to the wrong number:**

1. Complain to the sender company and bKash (16247) <sup>[N344](#n344), [150](#o150)</sup>.
2. Then complain to the company's complaint cell <sup>[150](#o150)</sup>.
3. Then complain to Bangladesh Bank's Customer Interest Protection Centre (16236 during office hours on working days, bb.cipc@bb.org.bd, or its online form) with a description, your name, address and phone, and the provider's final reply; a relative needs your written authorisation <sup>[150](#o150)</sup>.

<a id="going-home-without-valid-papers"></a>

## 15 Going home without valid papers

Without a valid iqama, the way out is the Bangladesh Embassy's Special Exit Programme (SEP), if your sponsor's labour office is covered (see item 1 below) <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. There is no time limit; workers have waited months, mostly without work <sup>[N347 (2023)](#n347)</sup>.

Overstaying any visa can bring a fine up to SR 50,000, up to 6 months in prison and deportation <sup>[151](#o151)</sup>. The re-entry ban, from departure, is usually 3 to 5 years for overstaying or huroob (the employer's report that a worker is absent from work) and 3 to 10 years for working on a tourist or Umrah visa <sup>[N151](#n151)</sup>. Leaving without a legitimate reason means paying your own ticket <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>.

**To leave through SEP:**

1. <a id="going-home-without-valid-papers-item-1"></a>**Check you qualify:**
   - You qualify if your sponsor's company is under the labour office of Riyadh, Al Qassim, Hail, Al Jouf, Northern Borders or the Eastern Province, and your iqama has expired, was never issued or carries a huroob report; house drivers and male domestic workers with an expired iqama also qualify <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
   - Excluded: women domestic workers; anyone who didn't leave on an earlier exit visa; anyone with a police or court case or wanted (matlub), who must settle it with that authority first <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
2. <a id="going-home-without-valid-papers-item-2"></a>**You'll need** <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>:
   - your iqama number;
   - your original iqama;
   - a passport copy showing its validity.
3. <a id="going-home-without-valid-papers-item-3"></a>**Apply online** at [bangladeshembassy.org.sa/sepsrv.html](https://www.bangladeshembassy.org.sa/sepsrv.html) ("Special Exit programme(SEP) Application Form"); no visit or call needed <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
4. <a id="going-home-without-valid-papers-item-4"></a>**Check progress** with your iqama number at [bangladeshembassy.org.sa/seppub](https://www.bangladeshembassy.org.sa/seppub/); when an appointment appears, print it and go to the embassy on that day with the documents <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
5. <a id="going-home-without-valid-papers-item-5"></a>**Get the exit visa:**
   - For a Riyadh company, the embassy gets labour office clearance; you then hand in your original iqama and passport copy, and the embassy sends them to Jawazat for the exit visa <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
   - For a company under another labour office, or a huroob case with a sponsor outside Riyadh, the embassy gives you a letter to take to that labour office or Jawazat, where you collect the exit visa <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does applying through SEP cost anything?
> - Must Saudi fines be paid before SEP issues an exit visa?
> - What can a worker do if an SEP application is refused?

**Sponsors under the Makkah, Madinah, Tabuk, Asir, Jazan, Najran or Al Baha labour office** aren't covered by SEP <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Contact the Jeddah consulate's labour wing ([Contacts](#contacts)) <sup>[152](#o152)</sup>. Workers seeking special exit, and domestic workers, call the consulate's toll-free hotline, 8002440051, from Jeddah and from outside it, to get the labour welfare wing's service <sup>[N81](#n81)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the exit procedure for a worker without valid papers whose sponsor is under the Makkah, Madinah, Tabuk, Asir, Jazan, Najran or Al Baha labour office?

**Women domestic workers,** excluded from SEP (item 1), can report their case on the labour wing's online form ([bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub](https://www.bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub/)), choosing the domestic worker problem type <sup>[55](#o55)</sup>; how they get an exit visa is not known. In May and June 2025 the expatriates' welfare adviser and his ministry said that, after his talks in Saudi Arabia, a chance had been created to regularise women workers living there illegally, giving no procedure, deadline or cost <sup>[N348](#n348), [N91](#n91)</sup>; the ministry's one-year report in August 2025 said measures had been taken to regularise irregular women workers in Saudi Arabia, Jordan and Oman <sup>[N92](#n92)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How does a woman domestic worker without valid papers get an exit visa?
> - How does a woman apply for the regularisation announced in 2025, and is it still open?

**Any other papers problem:**

1. Use the embassy's online form at [bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub](https://www.bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub/), choosing the problem (huroob, no iqama, in jail, salary, domestic worker), describing it in Bangla or English and attaching iqama and passport copies <sup>[55](#o55)</sup>.
2. If there's no reply, call the labour wing free on 8001000125 <sup>[79](#o79)</sup>.
3. Then have a relative write to the ministry (see [Help from Bangladesh](#help-from-bangladesh)) <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long does the embassy take to reply to the online request form?

**Without a passport** you need a travel permit (outpass) from the embassy's passport wing <sup>[N349](#n349), [79](#o79)</sup>. Either <sup>[79](#o79), [91](#o91)</sup>:

- call 8001000126 free; or
- book "Consular & Welfare Service" at [passportriyadh.com/appointment](https://passportriyadh.com/appointment/) with your name, phone, passport number and OID number.

In a deportation centre, see [If you are detained](#if-you-are-detained).

> **Need to know**
> - Which documents does the passport wing need for a travel permit?
> - What is the travel permit's fee?
> - What is the OID number, and where does a worker find it?
> - How does a worker book the appointment without a passport number?

<a id="help-from-bangladesh"></a>

### 15.1 Help from Bangladesh

A relative in Bangladesh can ask the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare, free, to have the worker brought home; within 7 working days the ministry asks the embassy, the Welfare Board or BMET to do it <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.

1. <a id="help-from-bangladesh-item-1"></a>**You'll need** <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>:
   - proof the person is a migrant worker (passport, BMET smart card, iqama or work permit copies);
   - the Saudi workplace and home addresses;
   - the agency's name and address;
   - the employer's mobile number.
